# Targum Jonathan on I Samuel

תרגום יונתן על שמואל א

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel)

תרגום מרכזי על ספרי הנביאים שנקרא בציבור בבית הכנסת בתקופת התלמוד, ועדיין נקרא היום בקהילות של יהודי תימן.

Primary Targum on the books of the Prophets, read publicly in synagogues in talmudic times and still today by Yemenite Jews.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Targum/Targum Jonathan/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Mikraot Gedolot (Segments: 811, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): The Targum of Samuel; trans. E. van Staalduine-Sulman. Leiden: Brill, 2002 (Segments: 811)


## Chapter 1

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1)


וַהֲוָה גַבְרָא חַד מֵרָמָתָא מִתַּלְמִידֵי נְבִיאַיָא מִטוּרָא דְבֵית אֶפְרָיִם וּשְׁמֵיהּ אֶלְקָנָה בַּר יְרֹחָם בַּר אֱלִיהוּא בַּר תּוֹחוּ בַר צוּף גַבְרָא פַּלֵיג חוּלַק בְּקוּדְשַׁיָא בְּטוּרָא דְבֵית אֶפְרָיִם:


There was a certain man of Ramah, of the pupils of the prophets, of the hill country of the House of Ephraim, whose name was Elkanah, son of Jeroham, son of Elihu, son of Tohu, son of Zuph, a man having a portion in the holy things in the hill country of the House of Ephraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:2)


וְלֵיהּ תַּרְתֵּין נְשִׁין שׁוּם חֲדָא חַנָה וְשׁוּם תִּנְיֵיתָא פְּנִנָה וַהֲוָה לִפְנִנָה בְּנִין וּלְחַנָה לֵית בְּנִין:


And he had two wives; the name of the one was Hannah, and the name of the other Peninnah. And Peninnah had sons, but Hannah had no sons.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:3)


וּסְלִיק גַבְרָא הַהוּא מִקַרְתֵּיהּ מִזְמַן מוֹעֵד לְמוֹעֵד לְמִסְגַד וּלְדַבָּחָא קֳדָם יְיָ צְבָאוֹת בְּשִׁלֹה וְתַמָן תְּרֵין בְּנֵי עֵלִי חָפְנִי וּפִנְחָס מְשַׁמְשִׁין קֳדָם יְיָ:


And that man used to go up from his city, periodically from festival to festival, to bow down and to sacrifice before the LORD Sebaoth at Shiloh, where the two sons of Eli, Hophni and Phinahas, were serving before the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:4)


וַהֲוָה יוֹם מוֹעֲדָא וְדַבַּח אֶלְקָנָה וִיהַב לִפְנִנָה אִתְּתֵיהּ וּלְכָל בְּנָהָא וּבְנָתָהָא חוּלָקִין:


And on the festival when Elkanah sacrificed, he would give portions to Peninnah his wife and to all her sons and daughters.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:5)


וּלְחַנָה יְהַב חוּלַק חָד בְּחִיר אֲרֵי יַת חַנָה רְחִים וּמִן קֳדָם יְיָ אִתְמְנַע מִנָהּ וְלָד:


And he would give to Hannah one choice portion, because he loved Hannah. And from before the LORD offspring was withheld from her.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6)


וּמְצַהֲבָא לָהּ צָרָתָהּ אַף מַרְגְזָא לָהּ בְּדִיל לְאַקְנָיוּתָהּ אֲרֵי אִתְמְנַע מִן קֳדָם יְיָ מִנָהּ וְלָד:


And her rival used to provoke her, also to anger her in order to make her jealous, because from before the LORD offspring was withheld from her.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7)


וְכֵן יַעֲבֵיד שְׁנָא בִּשְׁנָא בִּזְמַן מַסְקָהּ לְבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא דַייָ כֵּן מַרְגְזָא לָהּ וּבַכְיָא וְלָא אָכְלַת:


And so he did year by year; as often as she went up to the house of the LORD’s sanctuary, she used to anger her. And she was weeping and would not eat.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:8)


וַאֲמַר לָהּ אֶלְקָנָה בַּעֲלָהּ חַנָה לְמָה אַתְּ בַּכְיָא וּלְמָה לָא תֵיכְלִי וּלְמָה בָּאֵישׁ לִיךְ לִיבֵּךְ הֲלָא רְעוּתִי טָבָא לִיךְ מַעֲשְׂרָא בְּנִין:


And Elkanah, her husband, said to her, ‘Hannah, why are you weeping? And why did you not eat? And why is your heart sad to you? Is not my goodwill better to you than ten sons?’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9)


וְקָמַת חַנָה בָּתַר דְאָכְלַת בְשִׁלֹה וּבָתַר דִשְׁתִיאַת וְעֵלִי כַהֲנָא יָתֵיב עַל כּוּרְסֵיהּ עַל מְזוּזֵי בִּסְטַר סִיפָא דְהֵיכְלָא דַיְיָ:


And Hannah rose, after she had eaten in Shiloh and after they had drunk. Now Eli the priest was sitting on the seat beside the doorpost of the LORD’s temple.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:10)


וְהִיא מְרִירַת נְפָשׁ וּמְצַלְיָא קֳדָם יְיָ וּמִבְכָּא בַכְיָא:


And she was bitter of soul and she prayed before the LORD and wept abundantly.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11)


וְקַיָימַת קְיָים וַאֲמָרַת יְיָ צְבָאוֹת אִם מִגְלָא גְלֵי קֳדָמָךְ סִגוּף אַמְתָךְ וְיֵעוּל דָכְרָנִי קֳדָמָךְ וְלָא תַרְחִיק יַת אַמְתָךְ וְתִתֵּן לְאַמְתָךְ בַּר בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא וְאֶמְסְרִינֵיהּ דִיהֵי מְשַׁמֵשׁ קֳדָם יְיָ כָּל יוֹמֵי חַיוֹהִי וּמַרְוַת אֱנָשׁ לָא תְהֵי עֲלוֹהִי:


And she swore an oath and said, ‘O LORD Sebaoth, the affliction of thy maidservant is indeed revealed before Thee. And let my memory come in before Thee and do not keep thy maidservant far away. And give to thy maidservant a son in the midst of the sons of men. And I will hand him over, that he may serve before the LORD all the days of his life. And the dominion of man will not be upon him.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:12)


וַהֲוָה מִדְאַסְגִיאַת לְצַלָאָה קֳדָם יְיָ וְעֵלִי אוֹרִיךְ לָהּ עַד דְתִפְסוֹק:


And when she continued praying before the LORD, Eli was waiting for her until she would stop.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:13)


וְחַנָה הִיא מְצַלְיָא בְּלִבָּהּ לְחוֹד סִפְוָתָהָא נַיְידָן וְקָלָהּ לָא מִשְׁתְּמָע וְחָשְׁבָהּ עֵלִי כְּאִתְּתָא רַוְיָא:


And Hannah was praying in her heart. Only her lips moved, and her voice was not heard. And Eli took her to be as a drunken woman.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:14)


וַאֲמַר לָהּ עֵלִי עַד אֵימָתַי אַתְּ מִשְׁתַּטְיָא הֲלָא תְפִיגִין יַת חַמְרִיךְ מִנָיִךְ:


And Eli said to her, ‘How long will you act insane? Will you not let your wine evaporate from you?’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:15)


וַאֲתִיבַת חַנָה וַאֲמַרַת לָא רִבּוֹנִי אִתְּתָא עָקַת רוּחַ אֲנָא וַחֲמַר חֲדַת וְעַתִּיק לָא אִשְׁתֵּיתִי וַאֲמָרִית עוּלְבַן נַפְשִׁי בִּצְלוֹ קֳדָם יְיָ:


And Hannah answered and said, ‘No, my lord, I am a woman anguished of spirit. I have drunk neither new nor old wine, but I have told the humiliation of my soul in prayer before the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16)


לָא תַכְלִים יַת אַמְתָךְ קֳדָם בַּת רִשְׁעָא אֲרֵי מִסְגֵי אַקְנָיוּתִי וְאַרְגִיזוּתִי אוֹרְכִית בִּצְלוֹ עַד כְּעַן:


Do not rebuke your maidservant as a daughter of wickedness, for from the abundance of my jealousy and my anger I lingered in prayer until now.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:17)


וַאֲתֵיב עֵלִי וַאֲמַר אֱזִילִי לִשְׁלָם וֶאֱלָהָא יִשְׂרָאֵל יִתֵּן יַת בָּעוּתִיךְ דִבְּעִית מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי:


And Eli answered and said, ‘Go in peace, and the God of Israel grant you your petition which you have made from before Him.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:18)


וַאֲמַרַת תַּשְׁכַּח אַמְתָךְ רַחֲמִין בְּעֵינָךְ וַאֲזָלַת אִתְּתָא לְאוֹרְחָהּ וַאֲכָלַת וְאַפִין בִּישִׁין לָא הֲווֹ לָהּ עוֹד:


And she said, ‘Let your maidservant find favour in your sight.’ Then the woman went her way and ate, and her countenance was no longer sad.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:19)


וְאַקְדִימוּ בְצַפְרָא וּסְגִידוּ קֳדָם יְיָ וְתָבוּ וְאָתוֹ לְבֵיתְהוֹן לְרָמָתָא וִידַע אֶלְקָנָה יַת חַנָה אִתְּתֵיהּ וְעַל דָכְרָנָהּ קֳדָם יְיָ:


They rose early in the morning and bowed down before the LORD. Then they went back to their house, to Ramah. And Elkanah knew his wife Hannah and her memory went in before the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20)


וַהֲוָה לִזְמַן מִשְׁלַם יוֹמַיָא וְעַדִיאַת חַנָה וִילִידַת בַּר וּקְרַת יַת שְׁמֵיהּ שְׁמוּאֵל אֲרֵי אֲמָרַת מִן קֳדָם יְיָ שְׁאִלְתֵּיה:


And at the time of the completing of the days Hannah conceived and bore a son, and she called him name Samuel, for she said, ‘I have asked him from before the LORD.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21)


וּסְלִיק גַבְרָא אֶלְקָנָה וְכָל אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ לְדַבָּחָא קֳדָם יְיָ יַת דְבַח מוֹעֲדַיָא וּלְשַׁלָמָא יַת נִדְרֵיהּ:


And the man Elkanah and all the people of his house went up to sacrifice before the LORD the sacrifice of the festival, and to fulfil his vow.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22)


וְחַנָה לָא סְלִיקַת אֲרֵי אֲמָרַת לְבַעֲלָהּ עַד דְיִתְחַסֵיל רַבְיָא וְאַיְתִינֵיהּ וְאִתְחֲזֵי קֳדָם יְיָ וִיתֵיב תַּמָן עַד עָלְמָא:


But Hannah did not go up, for she said to her husband, ‘As soon as the boy is weaned, I will bring him that he be seen before the LORD and live there for ever.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:23)


וַאֲמַר לָהּ אֶלְקָנָה בַעֲלָהּ עֶבִידִי כִּדְתַקִין בְּעֵינַיְכִי אוֹרִיכִי עַד דְתַחְסְלִין יָתֵיהּ בְּרַם יְקַיֵם יְיָ יַת פִּתְגָמוֹהִי וְאוֹרִיכַת אִתְּתָא וְאוֹנִיקַת יַת בְּרָהּ עַד דַחֲסִילַת יָתֵיהּ:


And her husband Elkanah said to her, ‘Do what seems right in your sight; wait until you have weaned him. Only, may the LORD establish his words.’ So the woman waited and nursed her son, until she weaned him.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24)


וְאַסִקְתֵּיהּ עִמָהּ כַּד חֲסַלְתֵּיהּ בְּתוֹרִין תְּלָתָא וּמְכִילְתָא חֲדָא דְקִימְחָא וּגְרַב דַחֲמַר וְאַיְתִיתֵיהּ לְבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא דַייָ לְשִׁלוֹ וְרַבְיָא הֲוָה יָנִיק:


And when she had weaned him, she took him up with her, along with three bulls, one measure of flour and a skin of wine; and she brought him to the sanctuary of the LORD, to Shiloh. And the boy was young.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:25)


וּנְכִיסוּ יַת תּוֹרָא וְאַיְתִיאוּ יַת רַבְיָא לְוַת עֵלִי:


And they slaughtered the bull and brought the boy to Eli.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:26)


וַאֲמַרַת בְּבָעוּ רִבּוֹנִי חַיֵי נַפְשָׁךְ רִבּוֹנִי אֲנָא אִתְּתָא דְאִתְעַתָּדַת עִמָךְ הָכָא לְצַלָאָה קֳדָם יְיָ:


And she said, ‘Please, my lord! By your life, my lord, I am the woman who was standing here with you to pray before the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:27)


עַל רַבְיָא הָדֵין צַלֵיתִי וִיהַב יְיָ לִי יַת בָּעוּתִי דִבְעִית מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי:


Concerning this boy I prayed; and the LORD granted me my petition which I made from before Him.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 1:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28)


וְאַף אֲנָא מְסַרְתֵּיהּ דִיהֵי מְשַׁמֵשׁ קֳדָם יְיָ כָּל יוֹמַיָא דְהוּא קַיָם הוּא מְשַׁמֵשׁ קֳדָם יְיָ וּסְגִיד תַּמָן קֳדָם יְיָ:


And I have als handed him over that he may be serving before the LORD. All the days that he lives, he will serve before the LORD.’ And he bowed down before the LORD there.



## Chapter 2

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1)


וְצַלִיאַת חַנָה בְּרוּחַ נְבוּאָה וַאֲמַרַת כְּבַר שְׁמוּאֵל בְּרִי עֲתִיד לְמֶהֱוֵי נְבִיָא עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּיוֹמוֹהִי יִתְפָּרְקוּן מִידָא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וְעַל יְדוֹהִי יִתְעַבְּדַן לְהוֹן נִסִין וּגְבוּרָן בְּכֵן תְּקוֹף לִבִּי בְּחוּלְקָא דִיהַב לִי יְיָ וְאַף הֵימָן בַּר יוֹאֵל בַּר בְּרִי שְׁמוּאֵל עָתִיד דִיקוּם הוּא וְאַרְבַּעַת עֲשַׂר בְּנוֹהִי לְמֶהֱוֵי אָמְרִין בְּשִׁירָה עַל יְדֵי נִבְלִין וְכִנוֹרִין עִם אֲחֵיהוֹן לֵיוָאֵי לְשַׁבָּחָא בְּבֵית מַקְדְשָׁא בְּכֵן רָמַת קַרְנִי בְּמַתַּנְתָּא דְמַנִי לִי יְיָ וְאַף עַל פּוּרְעָנוּת נִסָא דַעֲתִיד לְמֶהֱוֵי בִּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי דַעֲתִידִין דְיֵיתוּן יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ בַּעֲגַלְתָּא חֲדַתָּא וְעִמֵיהּ קוּרְבַּן אֲשָׁמָא בְּכֵן תֵּימַר כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֶפְתָּח פּוּמִי לְמַלָלָא רַבְרְבָן עַל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבַי אֲרֵי חֲדִיתִי בְּפוּרְקָנָךְ:


And Hannah prayed in a prophetic spirit and said, ‘Now my son Samuel is to be a prophet over Israel. In his days they will be saved from the hand of the Philistines and by his hands miracles and mighty deeds will be done to them.’ Therefore, ‘my heart is strong in the portion that the LORD gave to me.’
‘And also Heman, son of Joel, son of my son Samuel, who is to stand up, he and his fourteen sons, to be speaking in song by means of lyres and lutes, together with their brothers, the Levites, to praise in the sanctuary.’ Therefore, ‘my horn is exalted in the gift that the LORD appointed to me.’
And furthermore, concerning the miraculous punishment which is to be among the Philistines, who are to bring the ark on a new cart and with it the guilt offering. Therefore, let the assembly of Israel say, ‘Let my mouth be opened to speak great things against my enemies, for I rejoiced in thy salvation.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:2)


עַל סַנְחֵרִיב מַלְכָּא דְאַתּוּב אִתְנַבִּיאַת וַאֲמַרַת עֲתִיד דִיקוּם הוּא וְכָל חֲיָלוּתֵיהּ עַל יְרוּשְׁלֵם וְנֵס סַגִי יִתְעֲבֵיד בֵּיהּ תַּמָן יִפְלוּן פִּגְרֵי מַשִׁרְיָתֵיהּ בְּכֵן יוֹדוּן כָּל עַמְמַיָא אוּמַיָא וְלִשָׁנַיָא וְיֵימְרוּן לֵית דְקַדִישׁ אֶלָא יְיָ אֲרֵי לֵית בַּר מִנָךְ וַעֲמָךְ יֵימְרוּן לֵית תַּקִיף אֶלָא אֱלָהָנָא:


Concerning Sennacherib, the king of Assyria, she prophesied and said that he was to come up against Jerusalem, he and all his armies. And a great miracle will be done to him: the corpses of his camps will fall there. Therefore, let all the nations, peoples and languages confess and say, ‘There is none who is holy but the LORD, for there is none besides Thee.’ And let your people say, ‘There is none who is strong but our God.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3)


עַל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַר מַלְכָּא דְבָבֶל אִתְנַבִּיאַת וַאֲמַרַת אַתּוּן כַּסְדָאֵי וְכָל עַמְמַיָא דַעֲתִידִין לְמִשְׁלַט בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל לָא תִסְגוּן לְמַלָלָא רַבְרְבָן לָא יִפְקוּן גִידוּפִין מִפּוּמְכוֹן אֲרֵי אֱלָהָא יְדַע כוֹלָא וְעַל כָּל עוֹבָדוֹהִי מְתִיחַ דִינֵיהּ אַף לְכוֹן עֲתִיד לְשַׁלָמָא פּוּרְעָנוּת חוֹבֵיכוֹן:


Concerning Nebuchadnezzar, the king of Babylon, she prophesied and said, ‘You, Chaldeans, and all the nations who are to rule in Israel, do not increase speaking boastful things and let no blasphemies go forth from your mouth, for the LORD is the God who knows everything and upon all his creations judgment is meted out. And also to you He is to make full requital for your guilt.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:4)


עַל מַלְכוּת מַקְדוֹן אִתְנַבִּיאַת וַאֲמַרַת קַשְׁתַּת דְגִבָּרֵי מִקְדוּנָאֵי יִתַּבְּרִין וּדְבֵית חַשְׁמוֹנָאֵי דַהֲווֹ חַלָשִׁין יִתְעַבְּדָן לְהוֹן נִסִין וּגְבוּרָן:


Concerning the kingdom of Greece she prophesied and said, ‘The bows of the Greek heroes will be broken and those of the Hasmonaean house who are weak, mighty deeds will be done for them.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5)


עַל בְּנוֹהִי דְהָמָן אִתְנַבִּיאַת וַאֲמַרַת דַהֲווֹ שָׂבְעִין בְּלַחְמָא וְגָאָן בְּעוּתְרָא וְסַגִיאִין בְמָמוֹנָא אִתְמַסְכְּנוּ תָּבוּ לְאִתַּגָרָא בִּלְחֵם וּמָזוֹן פּוּמְהוֹן מָרְדְכַי וְאֶסְתֵּר דַהֲווֹ חֲשִׁיכִין וּמִסְכְּנִין עֲתָרוּ וְאִיתְנְשִׁיאוּ יַת מִסְכְּנוּתְהוֹן תָּבוּ לְמֶהֱוֵי בְנֵי חוֹרִין כֵּן יְרוּשְׁלֵם דַהֲוַת כְּאִתְּתָא עֲקְרָא עֲתִידָא דְתִתְמְלֵי מֵעַם גַלְוָתָהָא וְרוֹמֵי דְמַלְיָא סַגִי עַמְמַיָא יְסוּפוּן מַשִׁרְיָתָהָא תְּצְדֵי וְתֵיחֲרוּב:


Concerning the sons of Haman she prophesied and said, ‘Those who were satisfied with bread and amassing riches and growing in money, will become poor. They have returned to hiring themselves out for bread, the food of their mouth. Mordecai and Esther, who were needy became rich and forgot their poverty. They have returned to being free persons. So Jerusalem which was like a barren woman is to be filled with her exiled people. And Rome which is filled with a mass of peoples, her armies will cease to be. She will be desolate and destroyed.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:6)


כָּל אִלֵין גְבוּרָתָא דַייָ דְהוּא שַׁלִיט בְעַלְמָא מֵמִית וַאֲמַר לְאַחָאָה וּמָחִית לִשְׁאוֹל וְאַף עֲתִיד לְאַסָקָא בְּחַיֵי עַלְמָא:


All these are a mighty work of the LORD, who is ruling in the world. He puts to death and promised to make alive. He brings down to Sheol and is also ro raise up in eternal life.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:7)


יְיָ מְמַסְכֵּין וּמְעַתֵּיר מַשְׁפִּיל אַף מֵרִים:


The LORD makes poor and makes rich; He brings low, He also exalts.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:8)


מֵקִים מֵעַפְרָא מִסְכֵּינָא מִקִלְקַלְתָּא מֵרִים חֲשִׁיכָא לְאוֹתָבוּתְהוֹן עִם צַדִיקַיָא רַבְרְבֵי עַלְמָא וְכוּרְסֵי יְקָרָא מַחֲסִין לְהוֹן אֲרֵי קֳדָם יְיָ גְלָן עוֹבָדֵי בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא מִלְרַע אַתְקֵין גֵהִנָם לְרַשִׁיעַיָא עָבְרֵי עַל מֵימְרֵיהּ וְצַדִיקַיָא עַבְדֵי רְעוּתֵיהּ שַׁכְלֵיל לְהוֹן תֵּבֵל:


He raises up the poor from the dust, from the ash heap he lifts the needy, to make them sit with the righteous, the chiefs of the world, and inherit the Throne of Glory. For before the LORD the deeds of the sons of men are revealed. He has established Gehenna below for the evil ones, and for the righteous ones, doing his will, He has completed the world.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:9)


וּגְוִיַת עַבְדוֹהִי צַדִיקַיָא יִטַר מִגֵהִנָם וְרַשִׁיעַיָא בְגֵהִנָם בַּחֲשׁוֹכָא יִתְדְנוּן לְהוֹדָעָא אֲרֵי לָא מַן דְאִית בֵּיהּ חֵילָא זַכֵּי לְיוֹם דִינָא:


He will keep the bodies of his righteous servants from Gehenna, but the evil ones will be judged in Gehenna, in darkness, to make it known that there is none in whom there is strength worthy for the day of judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10)


יְיָ יִתַבַּר בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָא דְקַיְמִין לְאַבְאָשָׁא לְעַמֵיהּ עֲלֵיהוֹן מִן שְׁמַיָא בְּקַל רָם יַשְׁקֵיף יְיָ יַעְבֵּיד פּוּרְעֲנוּת מִן מָגוֹג וּמִמַשִׁרְיַת עַמְמִין חֲטוּפִין דְאָתָן עִמֵיהּ מִסְיָפֵי אַרְעָא וְיִתֵּן תּוּקְפָא לְמַלְכֵּיהּ וִירַבֵּי מַלְכוּת מְשִׁיחֵיהּ:


The LORD will shatter the enemies who rise up to harm his people. Against them He will thunder from heaven with a loud voice. The LORD will make just requital to Gog and the armies of the robbing nations who come with him from the ends of the earth. And He will give strength to his king and enlarge the kingdom of his anointed one.’

וַאֲזַל אֶלְקָנָה לְרָמָתָא לְבֵיתֵיהּ וְרַבְיָא הֲוָה מְשַׁמֵשׁ קֳדָם יְיָ בְּחַיֵי עֵלִי כַהֲנָא:


And Elkanah went home to Ramah. And the boy served before the LORD, during the life of Eli the priest.

וּבְנֵי עֵלִי גַבְרִין רַשִׁעַיָא לָא הֲווֹ יָדְעִין לְמִדְחַל קֳדָם יְיָ:


And the sons of Eli were evil men. They did not know how to fear from before the LORD.

וְנִימוּסָא דְכַהֲנַיָא מִן עַמָא כָּל גְבַר דְנָכֵיס נִכְסְתָא וְאָתֵי עוּלֵימָא דְכַהֲנָא כִּמְבַשֵׁל בִּסְרָא וּמַשִׁילְיָא דִי לֵיהּ תְּלַת שְׁנִין בִּידֵיהּ:


And this was the priests’ due from the people: any man who slaughtered a sacrifice, the priest’s servant would come, while the meat was boiling, with a three-pronged fork in his hand,


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:14)


וּקְבַע לֵיהּ בְּכִיוֹרָא אוֹ בְדוּדָא אוֹ בִקְדֵירָא אוֹ בְמִלֵיסָא כֹּל דְמַסִיק מַשִׁלְיָא נְסִיב כַּהֲנָא לְנַפְשֵׁיהּ כְּדֵין עָבְדִין לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל דְאָתָן לְדַבָּחָא תַּמָן בְּשִׁלֹה:


and he would set it into the pan, or kettle, or cauldron, or pot; all that the fork brought up the priest would take for his living. So they did at Shiloh to all Israel who came there to offer.

And the boy Samuel was serving before the LORD during the life of Eli. And the word of the LORD was hidden in those days; no prophecy was revealed.

On that day Eli was lying down in his own place. His eyes had begun to grow dim so that he could not see.

And the lamp of the LORD’s sanctuary had not yet gone out, and Samuel was lying down in the court of the Levites. And a voice was heard from the LORD’s temple, where the ark of the LORD was.

And Samuel grew, and the speech of the LORD was in his aid, and none of all his words failed.

And all Israel, from Dan to Beersheba, knew that Samuel was reliable in the prophetic words of the LORD.

## Chapter 4

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:1)


וַהֲוָה פִּתְגָם שְׁמוּאֵל מַדְעָא לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל וּנְפַק יִשְׂרָאֵל לְקָדָמוּת פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי לְאַגָחָא קְרָבָא וּשְׁרוֹ עַל אָבֶן סְעָדָא וּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי שְׁרוֹ בַאֲפַק:


And the word of Samuel was pleasing to all Israel. Now Israel went out to wage war against the Philistines; they encamped at Stone of Help and the Philistines encamped at Aphek.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:2)


וּסְדָרוּ קְרָבָא פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי לָקֳדָמוּת יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִתְרְטִישׁוּ עָבְדֵי קְרָבָא וְאִתַּבָּרוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל קֳדָם פְּלִשְׁתָאֵי וּמְחוֹ בְסִידְרָא בְּחַקְלָא כְאַרְבְּעָא אַלְפִין גַבְרָא:


The Philistines drew up in line against Israel, and when the wagers of the battle spread, Israel was shattered before the Philistines. They slew about four thousand men in the battle line on the field.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:3)


וַאֲתָא עַמָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וַאֲמַרוּ סָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמָה תַבְּרָנָא יְיָ יוֹמָא דֵין קֳדָם פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי נִסַב לָנָא מִשִׁילֹה יַת אֲרוֹן קְיָמָא דַייָ וְיֵיתֵי בֵּינָנָא בְּדִיל יְקַר שְׁמֵיהּ נִתְפְּרִיק מִיַד בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָנָא:


And when the people came to the camp, the elders of Israel said, “Why has the LORD shattered us today before the Philistines? Let us take the ark of the covenant of the LORD from Shiloh to us, that he may go between us. On account of the Glory of his name we will be saved from the hand of our enemies.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:4)


וּשְׁלַח עַמָא לְשִׁילֹה וּנְטָלוּ מִתַּמָן יַת אֲרוֹן קְיָמָא דַייָ צְבָאוֹת דִשְׁכִינְתֵּהּ שַׁרְיָא עֵיל מִן כְּרוּבַיָא וְתַמָן תְּרֵין בְּנֵי עֵלִי עִם אֲרוֹן קְיָמָא דַייָ חָפְנִי וּפִינְחָס:


So the people sent to Shiloh, and they carried from there the ark of the covenant of the LORD *Sebaoth*, whose Presence dwells above the cherubim. And the two sons of Eli, Hophni and Phinehas, were there with the ark of the covenant of the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:5)


וַהֲוָה כַּד אֲתָא אֲרוֹן קְיָמָא דַייָ לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וְיַבִּיבוּ כָל יִשְׂרָאֵל יַבָּבָא רַבָּא וְאִשְׁתְּגָּשַׁת אַרְעָא:


When the ark of the covenant of the LORD came into the camp, all Israel gave a mighty shout, so that the earth resounded.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:6)


וּשְׁמָעוּ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי יַת קַל יַבָּבָא וַאַמַרוּ מָה קָל יַבָּבָא רַבָּא הָדֵין בְמַשְׁרִיתָא דִיהוּדָאֵי וִידָעוּ אֲרֵי אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ אָתָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא:


And when the Philistines heard the noise of the shouting, they said, “What is this noise of a great shouting in the camp of the Judaeans?” And they learned that the ark of the LORD had come to the camp.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:7)


וּדְחִילוּ פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי אֲרֵי אֲמַרוּ אֲתָא אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וַאֲמַרוּ וַי לָנָא אֲרֵי לָא הֲוַת כַּהֲדָא מֵאִיתְמְלֵי וּמִדְקָדָמוֹהִי:


And the Philistines were afraid, for they said, “The ark of the LORD has come to the camp.” And they said, “Woe to us! For it was not like this yesterday and before that.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:8)


וַי לָנָא מַן יְשֵׁיזְבִינָנָא מִיַד מֵימְרָא דַייָ דְעוֹבָדוֹהִי רַבְרְבִין הָאִלֵין אִלֵין אִינוּן גְבוּרָתָא דַייָ דִמְחָא יַת מִצְרַיִם בְּכָל מָחָא וּלְעַמֵיהּ עֲבַד פְּרִישָׁן בְּמַדְבְּרָא:


Woe to us! Who can deliver us from the hand of the speech of the LORD, whose works are great? These are the mighty deeds of the LORD, who smote the Egyptians with every plague, but did for his people wonders in the wilderness.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:9)


אִיתְקְפוּ וַהֲווֹ לְגַבְרִין פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי דִלְמָא תִּשְׁתַּעְבְּדוּן לִיהוּדָאֵי כְּמָא דְאִשְׁתַּעְבְּדוּ לְכוֹן וּתְהוֹן לְגַבְרִין גִבָּרִין וְתַגִיחוּן קְרָבָא:


Take courage and be heroic men, O Philistines, lest you become subjected to the Judaeans as they have been subjected to you. Be heroic men and wage war!”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:10)


וְאַגִיחוּ קְרָבָא פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וְאִתַּבָּרוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאַפָּכוּ גְבַר לְקִירְווֹהִי וַהֲוָה מְחָתָא רַבְּתָא לַחֲדָא וְאִתְקְטַל מִיִשְׂרָאֵל תְּלָתִין אַלְפִין גְבַר רִגְלָי:


And the Philistines did wage war. And Israel was shattered, and they retreated, every man to his city. And it was a very great slaughter, and there fell of Israel thirty thousand foot soldiers.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:11)


וַאֲרוֹנָא דַייָ אִשְׁתְּבֵי וּתְרֵין בְנֵי עֵלִי אִתְקְטָלוּ חָפְנִי וּפִינְחָס:


And the ark of the LORD was captured; and the two sons of Eli were killed, Hophni and Phinehas.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:12)


וּרְהַט גַבְרָא מִשִׁבְטָא דְבִנְיָמִן מִסִדְרָא וַאֲתָא לְשִׁלֹה בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא וּלְבוּשׁוֹהִי מְבַזְעִין וְעַפְרָא רְמֵי עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ:


And a man from the tribe of Benjamin’s House ran from the battle line and came to Shiloh the same day, with his clothes torn and earth thrown upon his head.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:13)


וַאֲתָא וְהָא עֵלִי יָתֵיב עַל כּוּרְסַיָא עַל כְּבַשׁ אוֹרַח תַּרְעָא מְסָכֵי אֲרֵי הֲוָה לִבֵּיהּ תְּבִיר עַל אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ וְגַבְרָא אָתָא לְחַוָאָה לְקַרְתָּא וְאִשְׁתְּגִישַׁת כָּל קַרְתָּא:


When he came, behold, Eli was sitting upon his seat on the path of the gateway, on the look out, for his heart trembled for the ark of the LORD. And when the man came to tell [the news] in the city, all the city was shaken.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:14)


וּשְׁמַע עֵלִי יַת קַל צַוְחָתָא וַאֲמַר מָה קַל שִׁגּוּשַׁיָא הָדֵין וְגַבְרָא אוֹחִי וְאָתָא וְחַוֵי לְעֵלִי:


When Eli heard the sound of the outcry, he said, “What is the sound of this panic?” Then the man hastened and came to tell Eli.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:15)


וְעֵלִי בַּר תִּשְׁעִין וְתַמְנֵי שְׁנִין וְעֵינוֹהִי קָמָה וְלָא יָכִיל לְמֶחֱזֵי:


Now Eli was ninety eight years old and his eyes were set, so that he could not see.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:16)


וַאֲמַר גַבְרָא לְעֵלִי אֲנָא אֲתֵית מִן סִדְרָא וַאֲנָא מִן סִדְרָא דִקְרָבָא אַפָּכִית יוֹמָא דֵין וַאֲמַר מֶה הֲוָה פִּתְגָמָא בְּרִי:


And the man said to Eli, “I came from the battle line, and I fled from the battle line today.” And he said, “What was the situation, my son?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:17)


וַאֲתֵיב דִמְבַשֵׂר וַאֲמַר אַפֵּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל קֳדָם פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וְאַף מְחָתָא רַבְּתָא הֲוַת בְּעַמָא וְאַף תְּרֵין בְּנָךְ אִתְקְטָלוּ חָפְנִי וּפִינְחָס וַאֲרוֹנָא דַייָ אִשְׁתְּבִי:


He who brought the tidings answered and said, “Israel has retreated from before the Philistines, and there has also been a great stroke among the people; your two sons also, Hophni and Phinehas, have been killed and the ark of the LORD has been captured.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:18)


וַהֲוָה כַּד אַדְכָּרוּתֵיהּ יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ וּנְפַל מֵעַל כּוּרְסָא לַאֲחוֹרוֹהִי עַל כְּבַשׁ אוֹרַח תַּרְעָא וְאִתַּבָּרַת פָּרְקוּתֵיהּ וּמִית אֲרֵי סִיב גַבְרָא וְיַקִיר וְהוּא דָן יַת יִשְׂרָאֵל אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין:


When he mentioned the ark of the LORD, Eli fell backward from his seat on the path of the gateway; and his neck was broken and he died, for he was an old man, and heavy. He had judged Israel forty years.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:19)


וְכַלָתֵיהּ אִתַּת פִּינְחָס מְעַדְיָא לְמֵילַד וּשְׁמָעַת יַת שְׁמוּעֲתָא דְאִשְׁתְּבִי אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ וּדְמִית חָמוּהָא וּדְאִתְקְטֵיל בַּעֲלָהּ וּכְרָעַת וִילֵידַת אֲרֵי בִעַתוּהָא חֶבְלָהָא:


Now his daughter in law, the wife of Phinehas, was with child, about to give birth. And when she heard the report that the ark of the LORD was captured, and that her father in law had died, and that her husband was killed, she bowed and gave birth, for her pains frightened her.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:20)


וּכְעִידַן מוּתָה וּמַלִילוּ נְשַׁיָא דְקַיְמָן עִלַוָה לָא תִדְחֲלִין אֲרֵי בַּר דְכַר יְלִידַת וְלָא אֲתִיבַת וְלָא שַׁוִיאַת עַל לִבָּהּ:


And about the time of her death the women standing around her said to her, “Fear not, for you have borne a male son.” But she did not answer or give heed.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:21)


וּקְרַת לְרַבְיָא אִי כָבוֹד לְמֵימַר גְלָה יְקָרָא מִיִשְׂרָאֵל דְאִשְׁתְּבִי אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ וּדְמִית חָמוּהָא וּדְאִתְקְטֵל בַּעֲלָהּ:


And she named the boy Ichabod, saying, “The Glory has been exiled from Israel!” for the ark of the LORD had been captured and her father in law had died and her husband had been killed.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 4:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:22)


וַאֲמָרַת גְלָה יְקָרָא מִיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲרֵי אִשְׁתְּבֵי אֲרוֹנָא דַיָי:


She had said, “The Glory has been exiled from Israel,” for the ark of the Lord has been captured.



## Chapter 5

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:1)


וּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי שְׁבוֹ יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ וְאַיְתוֹהִי מֵאֶבֶן סַעֲדָא לְאַשְׁדוֹד:


When the Philistines captured the ark of the LORD, they brought it from the Stone of Help to Ashdod.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:2)


וּנְסִיבוּ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ וְאָעִילוּ יָתֵיהּ לְבֵית דָגוֹן וַאֲקִימוּ יָתֵיהּ בִּסְטַר דָגוֹן:


Then the Philistines took the ark of the LORD and brought it into the house of Dagon and set it up beside Dagon.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:3)


וְאַקְדִימוּ אֱנָשֵׁי אַשְׁדוֹד בְּיוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹהִי וְהָא דָגוֹן רָמֵי עַל אַפּוֹהִי עַל אַרְעָא קֳדָם אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ וּנְסִיבוּ יַת דָגוֹן וַאֲתִיבוּ יָתֵיהּ לְאַתְרֵיהּ:


And when the people of Ashdod rose early the next day, behold, Dagon had fallen on his face to the ground before the ark of the LORD. So they took Dagon and put him back in his place.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:4)


וְאַקְדִימוּ בְצַפְרָא בְּיוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹהִי וְהָא דָגוֹן רָמֵי עַל אַפּוֹהִי עַל אַרְעָא קֳדָם אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ וְרֵישׁ דָגוֹן וְתַרְתֵּין פִּיסַת יְדוֹהִי קְצִיצָן מַחְתָא עַל סְקוּפְתָּא לְחוֹד גוּפֵיהּ אִשְׁתָּאַר עֲלוֹהִי:


When they rose early in the morning the next day, behold, Dagon had fallen on his face to the ground before the ark of the LORD. And the head of Dagon and the two palms of his hands were lying cut off upon the threshold; only his body was left to him.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:5)


עַל כֵּן לָא דָרְכִין כּוּמְרֵי דָגוֹן וְכָל דְעָלִין לְבֵית דָגוֹן עַל סְקוּפַת דָגוֹן בְּאַשְׁדוֹד עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין:


Therefore the idol priests of Dagon and all who enter the house of Dagon do not tread on the threshold of Dagon in Ashdod to this day.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:6)


וּתְקֵיפַת מָחָתָא דַייָ עַל אֱנַשׁ אַשְׁדוֹד וְאַצְדִינוּן וְאַלְקִי יַתְהוֹן בִּטְחוֹרִין יַת אַשְׁדוֹד וְיַת תְּחוּמָהָא:


And the stroke of the LORD was heavily upon the people of Ashdod, and He terrified and afflicted them with haemorrhoids, both Ashdod and its territory.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:7)


וַחֲזוֹ אֱנָשֵׁי אַשְׁדוֹד אֲרֵי שַׁוְיָא עֲלֵיהוֹן מְחָתָא וְאָמְרִין לָא יִשְׁרֵי אֲרוֹן אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל עִמָנָא אֲרֵי תְקִיפַת מְחָתֵיהּ עֲלָנָא וְעַל דָגוֹן טַעֲוָתָנָא:


And when the people of Ashdod saw that the stroke was equally upon them, they said, “The ark of the God of Israel must not dwell with us; for his stroke is heavily upon us and upon Dagon our idol.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:8)


וּשְׁלָחוּ וּכְנָשׁוּ יַת כָּל טוּרְנֵי פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי לְוַתְהוֹן וַאֲמָרוּ מָה נַעְבֵּיד לַאֲרוֹן אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲמָרוּ לְגַת יִסְתַּחַר אֲרוֹן אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְאַסְחָרוּ יַת אֲרוֹן אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


So they sent and gathered together all the chiefs of the Philistines, and said, “What shall we do with the ark of the God of Israel?” They said, “Let the ark of the God of Israel be brought around to Gath.” So they brought the ark of the God of Israel around.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:9)


וַהֲוָה בָּתַר דְאַסְחָרוּ יָתֵיהּ וַהֲוַת מְחָתָא דַייָ בְּקַרְתָּא שִׁגוּשָׁא רַבָּא לַחֲדָא וּמְחָא יַת אֱנָשֵׁי קַרְתָּא מִזְעֵירָא וְעַד רַבָּא וּלְקוֹ לְהוֹן בִּטְחוֹרִין:


But after they had brought it around, the stroke of the LORD was on the city, a very great panic. And He struck the men of the city, great and small, so that they were afflicted with haemorrhoids.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:10)


וּשְׁלָחוּ יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ לְעֶקְרוֹן וַהֲוָה כְּמֵיתֵי אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ לְעֶקְרוֹן וּזְעִיקוּ עֶקְרוֹנָאֵי לְמֵימָר אַסְחָרוּ לְוָתָנָא יַת אֲרוֹן אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל לְקַטָלוּתָנָא וְיַת עִמָנָא:


So they sent the ark of the LORD to Ekron. And when the ark of the LORD came to Ekron, the Ekronites cried out, “They have brought around to me the ark of the God of Israel to kill us and our people.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:11)


וּשְׁלַחוּ וּכְנָשׁוּ יַת כָּל טוּרְנֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וַאֲמַרוּ שַׁלְחוּ יַת אֲרוֹן אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וִיתוּב לְאַתְרֵיהּ וְלָא יִקְטוֹל יָתִי וְיַת עַמִי אֲרֵי הֲוַת שִׁיגוּשׁ קְטָלָא בְּכָל קַרְתָּא תְּקֵיפַת לַחֲדָא מֲחָתָא דַייָ תַּמָן:


Then they sent and gathered together all the chiefs of the Philistines, and said, “Send away the ark of the God of Israel, and let it return to its own place, that it may not kill me and my people.” For there was a deathly panic throughout the whole city. The stroke of the LORD was very heavy there.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:12)


וְגַבְרַיָא דִי לָא מִיתוּ לָקוּ בַּטְחוֹרִין וּסְלִיקַת צִוְחַת קַרְתָּא לְצֵית שְׁמַיָא:


The men who did not die were stricken with haemorrhoids, and the cry of the city went up toward heaven.



## Chapter 6

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:1)


וַהֲוָה אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ בְּקִרְוֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי שַׁבְעָא יַרְחִין:


And the ark of the LORD was in the cities of the Philistines seven months.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:2)


וּקְרוֹ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי לְכוּמָרַיָא וּלְקָסְמַיָא לְמֵימַר מָה נַעְבֵּיד לַאֲרוֹנָא דַייָ הוֹדִיעוּנָנָא בַּמֶה נְשַׁלְחִינֵיהּ לְאַתְרֵיהּ:


And the Philistines called for the priests and the diviners, saying, “What shall we do with the ark of the LORD? Let us know with what we shall send it to its place.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:3)


וַאֲמַרוּ אִם אַתּוּן מְשַׁלְחִין יַת אֲרוֹן אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל לָא תְשַׁלְחוּן יָתֵיהּ רֵיקָן אֱלָהֵין אֲתָבָא תְּתִיבוּן קֳדָמוֹהִי קֻרְבַּן אֲשָׁמָא בְּכֵן תִּתָּסוּן וְיִתְרְוַח לְכוֹן לְמָה לָא תְנוּחַ מְחָתֵיהּ מִנְכוֹן:


And they said, “If you send away the ark of the God of Israel, do not send it empty, for you shall by all means return before Him a guilt offering. Then you will be healed and it will be relieved to you. Why would his stroke not rest from you?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:4)


וַאֲמַרוּ מַה קֻרְבַּן אֲשָׁמָא דְנָתִיב קֳדָמוֹהִי וַאֲמַרוּ מִנְיַן טוּרְנֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי חַמְשָׁא טְחוֹרֵי דַהֲבָא וְחַמְשָׁא עַכְבְּרֵי דַהֲבָא אֲרֵי מְחָתָא חֲדָא שַׁוְיָא לְכוּלְהוֹן וּלְטוּרְנֵיכוֹן:


When they said, “What is the guilt offering that we shall return before Him?” they said, “The number of the chiefs of the Philistines: five golden haemorrhoids and five golden mice, for the one stroke was equally on all of you and on your chiefs.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:5)


וְתַעְבְּדוּן צַלְמֵי טְחוֹרֵיכוֹן וְצַלְמֵי עַכְבְּרֵיכוֹן דִמְחַבְּלִין יַת אַרְעָא וְתִתְּנוּן קֳדָם אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל יְקָרָא מָאִים תָּנוּחַ מְחָתֵיהּ מִנְכוֹן וּמִן טַעֲוַתְכוֹן וּמִן אַרְעֲכוֹן:


So you must make images of your haemorrhoids and images of your mice that ravaged the land, and you shall give glory before the God of Israel. Perhaps his stroke will rest from you and your idols and your land.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:6)


וּלְמָה תְיַקְרִין יַת לִבְּכוֹן כְּמָא דְיַקְרוּ מִצְרָאֵי וּפַרְעֹה יַת לִבְּהוֹן הֲלָא כַּד אִתְפְּרַע מִנְהוֹן וּבָתַר כֵן שַׁלְחִינוּן וַאֲזָלוּ:


Why should you harden your hearts as the Egyptians and the pharaoh hardened their hearts? Was that not when revenge was taken on them? And afterwards they sent them away and they went.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:7)


וּכְעַן סְבוּ וַעֲבִידוּ עֲגָלָתָא חַדְתָּא חֲדָא וְתַרְתֵּן תּוֹרָן מְיַנְקָן דִי לָא נְגַדָא בְּנִיר וְתֵיסְרוּן יַת תּוֹרָתָא בַּעֲגָלָתָא וּתְתִיבוּן בְּנֵיהֶן מִבַּתְרֵיהֶן לְגָיו:


Now then, take and prepare one new cart and two milch cows which have never pulled in a yoke. And bind the cows to the cart, but send their calves back inside, so that they cannot follow them.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:8)


וְתִסְבּוּן יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ וְתִתְּנוּן יָתֵיהּ בַּעֲגָלָתָא וְיַת מָנֵי דַהֲבָא דַאֲתֵיבְתּוּן קֳדָמוֹהִי קֻרְבַּן אֲשָׁמָא תְּשַׁווּן בְּתֵיבוּתָא מִסִטְרֵיהּ וְתִשְׁלְחוּן יָתֵיהּ וְיֵהָךְ:


And you shall take the ark of the LORD and put it down on the cart. And you shall place in a box at its side the golden figures which you are returning before Him as a guilt offering. Then you shall send it off, and it will go.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:9)


וְתֶחֱזוּן אִם בְּאוֹרַח תְּחוּמֵיהּ יִסַק לְבֵית שֶׁמֶשׁ מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי אִתְעֲבִידַת לָנָא יַת בִּישְׁתָא רַבְּתָא הָדָא וְאִם לָא וְנִידַע אֲרֵי לָא מְחָתֵיהּ קְרִיבַת בָּנָא עֵירוּעַ הוּא דַהֲוָה לָנָא:


And you shall watch: if it goes up on the way of its own territory, to Beth shemesh, then this great harm was done from before Him; but if not, then we shall know that it was not his stroke that came near to us, it happened to us by chance.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:10)


וַעֲבַדוּ גַבְרַיָא כֵּן וּנְסִיבוּ תַּרְתֵּין תּוֹרָן מְיַנְקָן וְאַסְרוּנוּן בַּעֲגָלָתָא וְיַת בְּנֵיהֶן כָּלוּ בְּבֵיתָא:


And the men did so, and led two milch cows and bound them to the cart, and shut up their calves at home.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:11)


וְשַׁוִיאוּ יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ בַּעֲגָלָתָא וְיַת תֵּיבוּתָא וְיַת עַכְבְּרֵי דַהֲבָא וְיַת צַלְמֵי טְחוֹרֵיהוֹן:


And they put the ark of the LORD on the cart, and the box and the golden mice and the images of their haemorrhoids.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:12)


וְאַכְוֵינָא תּוֹרָתָא בְּאָרְחָא עַל אוֹרַח בֵּית שֶׁמֶשׁ בִּכְבִישָׁא חֲדָא אָזְלִין מֵיזַל וְנָעַיָן וְלָא סְטָאָה לְיַמִינָא וְלִסְמְאָלָא וְטוּרְנֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי אָזְלִין בַּתְרֵיהוֹן עַד תְּחוּם בֵּית שֶׁמֶשׁ:


And the cows went straight on the way, in the direction of Beth shemesh, along one highway, lowing as they went. They turned neither to the right nor to the left, and the chiefs of the Philistines went after them as far as the border of Beth shemesh.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:13)


וּבֵית שֶׁמֶשׁ חַצְדִין חֲצַד חִטִין בְּמֵישְׁרָא וּזְקָפוּ יַת עֵינֵיהוֹן וַחֲזוֹ יַת אֲרוֹנָא וַחֲדִיאוּ לְמֶחְזֵי:


And Beth shemesh was harvesting the wheat harvest in the valley; and when they lifted up their eyes and saw the ark, they rejoiced to see it.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:14)


וַעֲגָלָתָא אָתַת לַחֲקַל יְהוֹשֻׁעַ דִי בְּבֵית שֶׁמֶשׁ וְקָמָא תַמָן וְתַמָן אַבְנָא רַבְּתָא וְצַלָחוּ יַת אֲעֵי עֲגָלָתָא וְיַת תּוֹרָתָא אַסִיקוּ עֲלָתָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


The cart went to the field of Joshua, who was of Beth shemesh, and stood there. And a great stone was there; and they split up the wood of the cart and sacrificed the cows as a burnt offering before the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:15)


וְלֵוָאֵי אָחִיתוּ יַת אָרוֹנָא דַייָ וְיַת תֵּיבוּתָא דִי עִמֵיהּ דִי בֵיהּ מָנֵי דַהֲבָא וְשַׁוִיאוּ עַל אַבְנָא רַבְּתָא וְגַבְרֵי בֵית שֶׁמֶשׁ אַסִיקוּ עֲלַוָן וּנְכִיסוּ נִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא קֳדָם יְיָ:


And the Levites took down the ark of the LORD and the box that was with it, in which were the golden things, and put them upon the great stone. And the men of Beth shemesh sacrificed burnt offerings and slaughtered holy sacrifices on that day before the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:16)


וְחַמְשָׁא טוּרְנֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי חֲזוֹ וְתָבוּ לְעֶקְרוֹן בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא:


And when the five chiefs of the Philistines saw it, they returned that day to Ekron.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:17)


וְאִלֵין טְחוֹרֵי דַהֲבָא דַאֲתִיבוּ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי קֻרְבַן אֲשָׁמָא קֳדָם יְיָ דְאַשְׁדוֹד חַד דְעַזָה חַד דְאַשְׁקְלוֹן חַד דְגַת חַד דְעֶקְרוֹן חַד:


These are the golden haemorrhoids which the Philistines returned as a guilt offering before the LORD: one of Ashdod, one of Gaza, one of Ashkelon, one of Gath, one of Ekron.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:18)


וְעַכְבְּרֵי דַהֲבָא מִנְיַן כָּל קִרְוֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי לְחַמְשָׁא טוּרְנַיָא מִקִירְוִין כְּרִיכָן וְעַד קִירְוֵי פַצְחַיָא וְעַד אַבְנָא רַבְּתָא דַאֲחִיתוּ עֲלָהּ יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין הָא הִיא בַּחֲקַל יְהוֹשֻׁעַ דְמִבֵּית שֶׁמֶשׁ:


And the golden mice, the number of all the cities of the Philistines belonging to the five chiefs, from the fortified cities unto the unwalled cities, and unto the great stone on which they returned the ark of the LORD, to this day. Behold, it is in the field of Joshua who was of Beth shemesh.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:19)


וּקְטַל בְּגַבְרֵי בֵית שֶׁמֶשׁ עַל דְחָדִיאוּ דַחֲזוֹ אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ כַּד גְלֵי וּקְטַל בְּסָבֵי עַמָא שַׁבְעִין גַבְרָא וּבִקְהָלָא חַמְשִׁין אַלְפֵי גַבְרָא וְאִתְאַבָּלוּ עַמָא אֲרֵי מְחָא יְיָ בְּעַמָא מָחָא סַגִיאָה:


And He killed among the men of Beth shemesh, because they rejoiced as they saw the ark of the LORD, when it was exposed; and He killed among the elders of the people seventy men and among the assembly fifty thousand men. And the people mourned, because the LORD had made a great stroke among the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:20)


וַאֲמָרוּ גַבְרֵי בֵית שֶׁמֶשׁ מַן יָכִיל לְמֵיקַם קֳדָם אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ אֱלֹהִים קַדִישָׁא הָדֵין וּלְאֵידֵין אֲתַר יִסְתַּלֵק מִנָנָא:


Then the men of Beth shemesh said, “Who is able to stand before the ark of the LORD, this holy God? And to what place shall it be brought up, away from us?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/6:21)


וּשְׁלָחוּ אִזְגַדִין לְוַת יַתְבֵי קִרְיַת יְעָרִים לְמֵימַר אָתִיבוּ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ חוּתוּ אַסִיקוּ יָתֵיהּ לְוַתְכוֹן:


So they sent messengers to the inhabitants of Kiriath jearim, saying, “The Philistines have returned the ark of the LORD. Come down and take it up to you.”



## Chapter 7

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:1)


וַאֲתוֹ גַבְרֵי קִרְיַת יְעָרִים וַאֲסִיקוּ יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ וְאָעִלוּ יָתֵיהּ לְבֵית אֲבִינָדָב דִי בְגִבְעָתָא וְיַת אֶלְעָזָר בְּרֵיהּ זַמִינוּ לְמִטַר יַת אֲרוֹנָא דַיָי:


And the men of Kiriath jearim came and took up the ark of the LORD and brought it to the house of Abinadab on the hill and they appointed his son, Eleazar, to guard the ark of the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:2)


וַהֲוָה מִיוֹמָא דִשְׁרָא אֲרוֹנָא בְּקִרְיַת יְעָרִים וּסְגִיאוּ יוֹמַיָא וַהֲווֹ עַסְרִין שְׁנִין וְאִתְנְהִיאוּ כָּל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל בָּתַר פּוּלְחָנָא דַיָי:


From the day that the ark lodged in Kiriath jearim many days passed – it became twenty years – and all the House of Israel gathered themselves after the worship of the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:3)


וַאֲמַר שְׁמוּאֵל לְכָל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֵימַר אִם בְכָל לִבְּכוֹן אַתּוּן תַּיְבִין לְפוּלְחָנָא דַייָ אַעֲדוּ יַת טַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָא מִבֵּינֵיכוֹן וְעַשְׁתְּרָתָא וְאַתְקִינוּ לִבְּכוֹן לְפֻלְחָנָא דַייָ וּפְלַחוּ קֳדָמוֹהִי בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי וִישֵׁיזֵב יַתְכוֹן מִידָא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי:


Then Samuel said to all the house of Israel, “If you are returning to the worship of the LORD with all your heart, then remove the idols of the nations and the Ashtaroth from among you, and direct your heart to the worship of the LORD and worship before Him alone. And He will deliver you from the hand of the Philistines.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:4)


וְאַעְדִיוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יַת בְּעָלַיָא וְיַת עַשְׁתְּרָתָא וּפְלַחוּ קֳדָם יְיָ בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי:


So Israel removed the Baals and the Ashtaroth and they worshipped before the LORD alone.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:5)


וַאֲמַר שְׁמוּאֵל כְּנִישׁוּ יַת כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִצְפַּיָא וַאֲצַלֵי עֲלֵיכוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ:


Then Samuel said, “Gather all Israel at Mizpah, and I will pray before the LORD on behalf of you.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:6)


וְאִתְכְּנִישׁוּ לְמִצְפַּיָא וּשְׁפִיכוּ לִבְּהוֹן בִּתְיוּבְתָּא כְּמַיָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְצָמוּ בְיוֹמָא הַהוּא וַאֲמַרוּ תַמָן חַבְנָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְדָן שְׁמוּאֵל יַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמִצְפַּיָא:


So they gathered themselves at Mizpah, and poured out their hearts like water, in repentance before the LORD. And they fasted on that day, and said there, “We are guilty before the LORD.” And Samuel judged the sons of Israel at Mizpah.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:7)


וּשְׁמָעוּ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי אֲרֵי אִתְכְּנִישׁוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִצְפַּיָא וּסְלִיקוּ טוּרְנֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל וּשְׁמָעוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּדְחִילוּ מִן קֳדָם פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי:


When the Philistines heard that the sons of Israel had gathered themselves at Mizpah, the chiefs of the Philistines went up against Israel. And when the sons of Israel heard of it, they feared before the Philistines.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:8)


וַאֲמַרוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לִשְׁמוּאֵל לָא תַשְׁתִּיק מִנָנָא מִלְצַלָאָה קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא וִיִפְרְקִנָנָא מִידָא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי:


And the sons of Israel said to Samuel, “Do not fall silent in your prayer for us before the LORD our God, that He may save us from the hand of the Philistines.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:9)


וּנְסִיב שְׁמוּאֵל אִמְרָא דַחֲלָבָא חַד וְאַסְקֵיהּ עֲלָתָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְצַלִי שְׁמוּאֵל קֳדָם יְיָ עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְקַבֵּיל צְלוֹתֵיהּ יְיָ:


So Samuel took one sucking lamb and sacrificed it as a whole burnt offering before the LORD. And Samuel prayed before the LORD on behalf of Israel and the LORD accepted his prayer.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:10)


וַהֲוָה שְׁמוּאֵל מַסִיק עֲלָתָא וּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי אִתְקְרָבוּ לְאַגָחָא קְרָבָא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל וְאַכְלִי יְיָ בְּקַל רַב בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא עַל פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וְשַׁגֵישִׁינוּן וְאִתַּבָּרוּ קֳדָם יִשְׂרָאֵל:


As Samuel was sacrificing up the burnt offering, the Philistines drew near to wage war against Israel. But the LORD thundered with a great voice that day against the Philistines and confused them; and they were shattered before Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:11)


וּנְפַקוּ אֱנָשֵׁי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִן מִצְפַּיָא וּרְדָפוּ יַת פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וּמְחוֹנוֹן עַד מִלְרַע לְבֵית שָׁרוֹן:


And the men of Israel went out of Mizpah and pursued the Philistines, and smote them, as far as below Beth sharon.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:12)


וּנְסִיב שְׁמוּאֵל אַבְנָא חֲדָא וְשַׁוִי בֵּין מִצְפַּיָא וּבֵין שִׁינָא וּקְרָא יַת שְׁמָהּ אֶבֶן סַעֲדָא וַאֲמַר עַד הָכָא סַעֲדָנָא יְיָ:


Then Samuel took one stone and set it up between Mizpah and Shen and called its name “Stone of Help”, for he said, “Up to here the LORD has helped us.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:13)


וְאִתְבָּרוּ פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וְלָא אוֹסִיפוּ עוֹד לְמֵיעַל בִּתְחוּם יִשְׂרָאֵל וַהֲוַת מְחָתָא דַייָ בִּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי כָּל יוֹמֵי שְׁמוּאֵל:


So the Philistines were shattered and did not again cross the border of the land of Israel. And the stroke of the LORD was on the Philistines all the days of Samuel.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:14)


וְתָבָא קִירְוַיָא דִנְסִיבוּ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי מִן יִשְׂרָאֵל לְיִשְׂרָאֵל מֵעֶקְרוֹן וְעַד גַת וְיַת תְּחוּמֵיהוֹן שֵׁיזִיב יִשְׂרָאֵל מִידָא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וַהֲוָה שְׁלָמָא בֵּין יִשְׂרָאֵל וּבֵין אֱמוֹרָאֵי:


The cities which the Philistines had taken from Israel were restored to Israel, from Ekron to Gath; and Israel delivered them from the hand of the Philistines. And there was peace between Israel and the Amorites.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:15)


וְדָן שְׁמוּאֵל יַת יִשְׂרָאֵל כֹּל יוֹמֵי חַיוֹהִי:


Samuel judged Israel all the days of his life.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:16)


וַאֲזַל בִּזְמַן שְׁנָא בִּשְׁנָא וּמִסְתְּחַר לְבֵית אֵל וּלְגִלְגָלָא וּלְמִצְפַּיָא וְדָאִין יַת יִשְׂרָאֵל יַת כָּל אַתְרַיָא הָאִלֵין:


And he used to go on a circuit year by year to Bethel, Gilgal, and Mizpah; and he was judging Israel, all these places.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/7:17)


וּבְמִתְבֵיהּ לְרָמָתָא אֲרֵי תַמָן בֵּיתֵיהּ וְתַמָן דָן יַת יִשְׂרָאֵל וּבְנָא תַמָן מִדְבְּחָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


And on his return to Ramah – for there was his house and there he judged Israel – he built there an altar before the LORD.



## Chapter 8

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:1)


וַהֲוָה כַּד סִיב שְׁמוּאֵל וּמַנִי יַת בְּנוֹהִי דַיָנִין עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


When Samuel became old, he appointed his sons as judges over Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:2)


וַהֲוָה שׁוּם בְּרֵיהּ בּוּכְרֵיהּ יוֹאֵל וְשׁוּם תִּנְיָנֵיהּ אֲבִיהָ דַיָנִין בִּבְאֵר שָׁבַע:


The name of his firstborn son was Joel, and the name of his second Abijah; they were judges in Beersheba.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:3)


וְלָא הֲלִיכוּ בְנוֹהִי בְּאוֹרְחָתֵיהּ וְאִתְפְּנִיאוּ בָּתַר מָמוֹן דִשְׁקָר וְקַבִילוּ שׁוֹחֲדָא וְצַלוּ דִינָא:


Yet his sons did not walk in his ways, but turned aside after false riches. They accepted bribes and perverted justice.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:4)


וְאִתְכְּנִישׁוּ כָּל סָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲתוֹ לְוַת שְׁמוּאֵל לְרָמָתָא:


Then all the elders of Israel gathered together and came to Samuel at Ramah.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:5)


וַאֲמַרוּ לֵיהּ הָא אַתְּ סִבְתָּא וּבְנָךְ לָא הֲלִיכוּ בְּאָרְחָתָךְ כְּעַן מַנֵי לָנָא מַלְכָּא לְמִדַיְנָנָא כְּכָל עַמְמַיָא:


And they said to him, “Behold, you have become old and your sons do not walk in your ways; now appoint for us a king to judge us like all the nations.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:6)


וּבְאֵישׁ פִּתְגָמָא בְּעֵינֵי שְׁמוּאֵל כַּד אַמַרוּ מַנֵי לָנָא מַלְכָּא לְמִדַיְנָנָא וְצַלֵי שְׁמוּאֵל קֳדָם יְיָ:


But the thing was evil in Samuel’s sight, when they said, “Appoint for us a king to judge us.” And Samuel prayed before the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:7)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לִשְׁמוּאֵל קַבֵּיל לְמֵימָר עַמָא לְכֹל דְיֵמְרוּן לָךְ אֲרֵי לָא יָתָךְ רְחִיקוּ אֲרֵי בְּפוּלְחָנִי קָצוּ מִלְמֶהֱוֵי מַלְכָּא עֲלֵיהוֹן:


And the LORD said to Samuel, “Accept the speech of the people in all that they say to you, for it is not you they have rejected, for they have refused my worship so that I cannot be king over them anymore.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:8)


כְּכָל עוֹבָדַיָא דִי עֲבַדוּ מִיוֹמָא דְאַסְקֵית יַתְהוֹן מִמִצְרַיִם וְעַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין וּשְׁבָקוּ פּוּלְחָנִי וּפְלָחוּ לְטַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָא כֵּן אִינוּן עָבְדִין אַף לָךְ:


According to all the deeds which they have done, from the day I brought them up out of Egypt to this day – they have forsaken my worship and have worshipped the idols of the nations so they are also doing to you, too.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:9)


וּכְעַן קַבֵּיל לְמֵימְרֵיהוֹן בְּרַם אֲרֵי אַסְהָדָא תַסְהִיד בְּהוֹן וּתְחַוֵי לְהוֹן נִימוּסָא דְמַלְכָּא דְיִמְלַךְ עֲלֵיהוֹן:


Now then, accept their speech. Only, you shall solemnly warn them, and tell them the right of the king who shall reign over them.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:10)

וַאֲמַר דֵין יְהֵי נִימוּסָא דְמַלְכָּא דִי מְלַךְ עֲלֵיכוֹן יַת בְּנֵיכוֹן יַדְבֵּר וִימַנֵי לֵיהּ בִּרְתִיכוֹהִי וּבְפָרָשׁוֹהִי וְרָדְפִין קֳדָם רְתִיכוֹהִי:


He said, “This is the right of the king who shall reign over you: he will take your sons and appoint them to his chariots and to be his horsemen and the pursuers before his chariots;


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/8:12)


וּלְמַנָאָה לֵיהּ רַבָּנֵי אַלְפִין וְרַבָּנֵי חַמְשִׁין וְאִכָּרַיָא לְמִיגַר נִירֵיהּ וּלְמֶחְצַד חַצְדֵיהּ וְאוּמְנַיָא לְמֶעְבַּד מָאנֵי קְרָבֵיהּ וּמָאנֵי רְתִיכוֹהִי:


and to appoint for himself commanders of thousands and commanders of fifty; and farmers to plough his ground and to reap his harvest; and craftsmen to make his implements of war and the equipment for his chariots.

He will take your daughters to be servants and cooks and bakers.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 8:14

He will take the best of your fields and vineyards and olive orchards and give them to his servants.

וְזַרְעֵיכוֹן וְכַרְמֵיכוֹן יִסַב חַד מִן עַסְרָא וִיהַב לְרַבְרְבוֹהִי וּלְעַבְדוֹהִי:


He will take one tenth of your grain and of your vineyards and give it to his commanders and his servants.

וְיַת עַבְדֵיכוֹן וְיַת אַמְהַתְכוֹן וְיַת עוּלֵימְכוֹן שַׁפִּירַיָא וְיַת חֲמָרֵיכוֹן יְדַבֵּר לְמֶהֱוֵי עָבְדִין עֲבִידְתָּא:


The most handsome of your menservants, your maidservants, your young men and your asses he will take for his business.

עַנְכוֹן יִסַב חָד מִן עַסְרָא וְאַתּוּן תְּהוֹן לֵיהּ לְעַבְדִין:


He will take one tenth of your flocks, and you will be his servants.

וּתְצַוְחוּן בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא מִן קֳדָם מַלְכְּכוֹן דְאִתְרְעִיתוּן לְכוֹן וְלָא יְקַבֵּיל יְיָ צְלוֹתְכוֹן בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא:


And in that day you will cry out because of your king, whom you have chosen for yourselves, but the LORD will not accept your prayer in that day.”

But the people rebelled against accepting Samuel’s speech and they said, “No! but there will be a king over us.

Then also we will be like all the nations. Our king will avenge us and go out at our head and wage our wars.”

And when Samuel had heard all the words of the people, he reported them before the LORD.

## Chapter 9

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:1)


וַהֲוָה גַבְרָא מִשִׁבְטָא דְבֵית בִּנְיָמִין וּשְׁמֵיהּ קִישׁ בַּר אֲבִיאֵל בַּר צְרוֹר בַּר בְּכוֹרַת בַּר אֲפִיחַ בַּר גַבְרָא מִשִׁבְטָא דְבֵית בִּנְיָמִן גִבַּר חֵילָא:


There was a man of the tribe of the House of Benjamin, whose name was Kish, the son of Abiel, son of Zeror, son of Becorath, son of Aphiah, son of a man from the tribe of the House of Benjamin, a mighty hero.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:2)


וְלֵיהּ הֲוָה בַּר וּשְׁמֵיהּ שָׁאוּל עוּלֵים וְשַׁפִּיר וְלֵית גַבְרָא מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל שַׁפִּיר מִנֵיהּ מִכַּתְפֵיהּ וּלְעֵלָא רָם מִכָּל עַמָא:


And he had a son whose name was Saul, a handsome young man. There was not a man among the sons of Israel who was more handsome than he. From his shoulder upward he was taller than all the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:3)


וַאֲבָדָא אֲתָנַיָא לְקִישׁ אָבוּהִי דְשָׁאוּל וַאֲמַר קִישׁ לְשָׁאוּל בְּרֵיהּ דְבַר כְּעַן עִמָךְ יַת חַד מֵעוּלֵמַיָא וְקוּם אֱזִיל בָּעֵי יַת אֲתָנַיָא:


Now the asses of Kish, Saul’s father, were lost. So Kish said to Saul his son, “Take one of the servants with you, and arise, go in search of the asses.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:4)


וַעֲבַר בְּטוּרָא דְבֵית אֶפְרַיִם וַעֲבַר בְּאַרַע דָרוֹמָא וְלָא אַשְׁכָּחוּ וַעֲבַר בְּאַרַע מַתְבְּרָא וְלֵית וַעֲבַר בְּאַרַע שִׁבְטָא בִּנְיָמִין וְלָא אַשְׁכָּחוּ:


And he passed through the hill country of the House of Ephraim and passed through the land of the South, but they did not find them. And they passed through the Land of the Birth stool, but they were not there. Then he passed through the land of Benjamin, but they did not find them.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:5)


אִינוּן אָתוֹ בְּאַרְעָא דְבָּהּ נְבִיָא דַייָ וְשָׁאוּל אֲמַר לְעוּלֵימָא דְעִמֵיהּ אִיתָא וְנֵיתוּב דִלְמָא יִשְׁבּוֹק אַבָּא מִן עֵיסַק אֲתָנַיָא וְיַצִיף לָנָא:


When they came to the land in which there was a prophet, Saul said to his young man, who was with him, “Come, let us go back, lest my father leave the matter of the asses and become anxious about us.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:6)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ הָא כְעַן נְבִיָא דַייָ בְּקַרְתָּא הָדָא וְגַבְרָא מִתְנַבֵּי קְשׁוֹט כָּל דְאִתְנַבֵּי אִתְקַיְמָא יִתְקַיֵם כְּעַן נֵיזַיל לָנָא תַמָן מָאִים יְחַוֵי לָנָא יַת אָרְחָנָא דַאֲתָנָא עֲלָהּ:


But he said to him, “Behold now, there is a prophet of the LORD in this city, and he is a man prophesying the truth; all that he prophesies will certainly be fulfilled. Now then, let us go there; perhaps he can tell us about our ways on which we have gone.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:7)


וַאֲמַר שָׁאוּל לְעוּלֵימֵיהּ וְהָא נֵיזִיל וְאִם מְקַבֵּל מָמוֹן מָה נָעֵיל לִנְבִיָא דַייָ אַף אֲנַחְנָא זְוָדִין עֲדַן עֲטָרוּ מִנָנָא וּמִדַעַם דְכָשַׁר לֵית לְאַעָלָא לִנְבִיָא דַייָ וּמָה עִמָנָא לְמֶעְבַּד:


Then Saul said to his young man, “Suppose we go, if he isused to accepting money, what can we bring the prophet of the LORD Moreover, our own provision ceased from our sacks, and there is nothing fit to bring to the prophet of the LORD What is there with us to do?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:8)


וְאוֹסִיף עוּלֵימָא לְמַעֲנֵי יַת שָׁאוּל וַאֲמַר הָא אִשְׁתְּכַח בִּידִי זוּזָא חֲדָא דְכַסְפָּא וְנִתֵּן לִנְבִיָא דַייָ וִיחַוֵי לָנָא יַת אָרְחָנָא:


And again the servant answered Saul, “Behold, I have in my hand one silver zuz, and I can give it to the prophet of the LORD, that he may tell us our way.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:9)


בְּקַדְמִין בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל כִּדְנַן אֲמַר גַבְרָא בְּמֵיזְלֵיהּ לְמִתְבַּע אוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אִיתוֹ וְנִתְמְטֵי עַד חֶזְוַיָא אֲרֵי לִנְבִיָא יוֹמָא דֵין מִתְקְרֵי מִלְקַדְמִין חֶזְוַיָא:


Formerly in Israel, when a man went to seek instruction from before the LORD he said it like this, “Come and let us meet the seer.” For he who is called a prophet today was formerly called a seer.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:10)


וַאֲמַר שָׁאוּל לְעוּלֵימֵיהּ תַּקִין פִּתְגָמָךְ אִיתָא נֵיזֵיל וַאֲזַלוּ לְקַרְתָּא דְתַמָן נְבִיָא דַיָי:


And Saul said to his young man, “Your word is right; come, let us go.” So they went to the city where the prophet of the LORD was.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:11)


אִינוּן סַלְקִין בְּמַסְקָנָא דְקַרְתָּא וְאִינוּן אַשְׁכָּחוּ עוּלֵימָן נַפְקִין לְמִמְלֵי מַיָא וַאֲמַרוּ לְהֵן הֲאִית הָכָא חֶזְוַיָא:


As they went up by the ascent of the city, they met young women coming out to draw water, and said to them, “Is the seer here?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:12)


וַאֲתִיבוּ יַתְהוֹן וַאַמָרָא אִית דָא הָא קֳדָמָךְ אוֹחִי כְעַן אֲרֵי יוֹמָא דֵין עַל לְקַרְתָּא אֲרֵי שֵׁירוּ נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא יוֹמָא דֵין לְעַמָא בְּבֵית אַסְחֲרוּתָא:


They answered them and said, “He is here, just ahead of you. Make haste, for today he has entered the city, because there is a meal with the holy slaughterings for the people today in the banqueting hall.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:13)


כְּמֵיעַלְכוֹן לְקַרְתָּא כֵּן תִּשְׁכְּחוּן יָתֵיהּ עַד לָא יִסַק לְבֵית אַסְחֲרוּתָא לְמֵיכַל אֲרֵי לָא אָכִיל עַמָא עַד מֵיתוֹהִי אֲרֵי הוּא פָרִיס מְזוֹנָא בָּתַר כֵּן אָכְלִין זַמְנַיָא וּכְעַן סַקוּ אֲרֵי יָתֵיהּ כְּיוֹמָא דֵין תִשְׁכְּחוּן יָתֵיהּ:


As soon as you enter the city, you will find him, before he goes up to the banqueting hall to eat; for the people will not eat till he comes, since he [always] says the benediction over the food; afterward those eat who are invited. Now, go up, for you can find him at this time.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:14

So they went up to the city. As they were entering the city, behold, Samuel was coming out toward them on his way up to the banqueting hall.
