And from before the LORD it was said to Samuel, one day before Saul’s coming,


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:16)


כְּעִידַן הָדֵין מְחָר אֶשְׁלַח לְוָתָךְ גַבְרָא מֵאַרְעָא שֵׁבֶט בִּנְיָמִין וְתִמְשְׁחִינֵיהּ לְמֶהֱוֵי מַלְכָּא עַל עַמִי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִפְרוֹק יַת עַמִי מִידָא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי אֲרֵי גְלֵי קֳדָמַי דוֹחֲקָא דְעַמִי אֲרֵי עֲלַת קְבֵילְתֵּהוֹן לָקֳדָמָי:


“Tomorrow about this time I will send to you a man from the land of the tribe of Benjamin, and you shall anoint him to be king over my people Israel. He shall save my people from the hand of the Philistines, for the oppression of my people is revealed before Me, for their complaint has come before Me.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:17)


וּשְׁמוּאֵל חֲזָא יַת שָׁאוּל וּמִן קֳדָם יְיָ אִתְאַמַר לֵיהּ הָא גַבְרָא דַאֲמָרִית לָךְ דֵין יַעְדֵי מַרְוָתָא מִן עַמִי:


When Samuel saw Saul, from before the LORD it was said to him, “Behold, the man of whom I spoke to you. He is it who will take away the dominion from my people.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:18)


וַעֲרַע שָׁאוּל יַת שְׁמוּאֵל בְּגוֹ תַרְעָא וַאֲמַר חַוֵי כְעַן לִי אֵי דֵין בֵּית חָזְוַיָא:


Then Saul met Samuel in the gate, and said, “Tell me where is the house of the seer?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:19

וְאָתֵיב שְׁמוּאֵל יַת שָׁאוּל וַאֲמַר אֲנָא חֶזְוַיָא סַק לָקֳדָמַי לְבֵית אַסְחֲרוּתָא וְתֵיכְלוּן עִמִי יוֹמָא דֵין וְאֶשְׁלְחִינָךְ בְּצַפְרָא וְכָל דִי בְּלִבָךְ אֲחַוֵי לָךְ:


Samuel answered Saul, “I am the seer; go up before me to the banqueting hall. Today you shall eat with me, and in the morning I will let you go and tell you all that is on your mind.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:20)


וְעַל עֵסֶק אֲתָנַיָא דַאֲבָדָא לָךְ וְאַתְּ אֲתֵיתָא לְמִבְעֵיהוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין דְנַן תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין לָא תְשַׁוֵי יַת לִבָּךְ עֲלֵיהוֹן אֲרֵי אִשְׁתְּכָחָא וּדְמַן כָּל חֶמְדַת יִשְׂרָאֵל הֲלָא דִי לָךְ וּלְכָל בֵּית אֲבוּךְ:


As for the matter of the asses that were lost to you, and which you came to search – today for three days – do not set your mind on them, for they have been found. But whose is all the delight of Israel? Is it not yours and all the house of your father?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:21)


וְאָתֵיב שָׁאוּל וַאֲמַר הֲלָא בַּר שֵׁבֶט בִּנְיָמִן אֲנָא מִזְעִירֵי שִׁבְטַיָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְזַרְעִיתִי חַלָשָׁא מִכָּל זַרְעֲיַת שֵׁבֶט בִּנְיָמִן וּלְמָא מַלֶלְתָּא עִמִי כְּפִתְגָמָא הָדֵין:


Saul answered, “Am I not a son of the tribe of Benjamin, from the least of the tribes of Israel? And is not my family the weakest of all the families of the tribe of Benjamin? Why then have you spoken with me in this way?”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:22)

Then Samuel led Saul and his young man and brought them to the meal and gave them a place at the head of those who had been invited, who were about thirty persons.

And Samuel said to the cook, “Bring the portion I gave to you, of which I said to you, ‘Keep it with you.’”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/9:24)


וַאֲרִים טַבָּחָא יַת שָׁקָא וְיַרְכֵיהּ וְשַׁוֵי קֳדָם שָׁאוּל וַאֲמַר הָא דְאִשְׁתָּאַר שַׁוִי קֳדָמָךְ אֲכוֹל אֲרֵי לְזִימְנָא נְטִיר לָךְ לְמֵימַר עַמָא זַמֵנִית לְשֵׁירוּתָא וַאֲכַל שָׁאוּל עִם שְׁמוּאֵל בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא:


So the cook took up the shank and the loin and set them before Saul. And he said, “See, what was left over is set before you. Eat, for it was kept for you for the time [I would] say, ‘I have invited the people to the meal.’” So Saul ate with Samuel on that day.

When they came down from the banqueting hall into the city, he spoke with Saul on the roof.

וְאַקְדִימוּ וַהֲוָה כְמֵסַק צַפְרָא וּקְרָא שְׁמוּאֵל לְשָׁאוּל לְאִגְרָא לְמֵימַר קוּם וְאֶשְׁלְחִינָךְ וְקָם שָׁאוּל וּנְפָקוּ תַרְוֵיהוֹן הוּא וּשְׁמוּאֵל לְבָרָא:


And they got up early. Then at the break of dawn Samuel called to Saul upon the roof, “Rise up, and I will send you off.” So Saul arose, and both he and Samuel went outside.

As they were going down to the outskirts of the city, Samuel said to Saul, “Say to your young man that he must pass on before us,” – and he passed on – “And you, stand here this moment, that I may make you hear the word of the LORD.”



## Chapter 10

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:1)


וּנְסִיב שְׁמוּאֵל יַת מָנָא דְמִשְׁחָא וַאֲרֵיק עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ וְנַשְׁקֵיהּ וַאֲמַר הֲלָא אֲרֵי מַשְׁחָךְ יְיָ עַל אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ לְמֶהֱוֵי מַלְכָּא:


Then Samuel took a vessel of oil and poured it on his head, and he kissed him and said, “Has not the LORD anointed you to be the king over his inheritance?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:2)


בְּמַהֲכָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין מִנִי מִלְוָתִי וְתַשְׁכַּח תְּרֵין גַבְרִין עִם קְבוּרְתָּא דְרָחֵל בִּתְּחוּם בִּנְיָמִן בְּצֶלְצַח וְיֵימְרוּן לָךְ אִשְׁתְּכָחָא אֲתָנַיָא דַאֲזַלְתָּ לְמִבְעֵי יַתְהֶן וְהָא שְׁבַק אֲבוּךְ יַת עֵיסַק אֲתָנַיָא וְיַצִיף לְכוֹן לְמֵימַר מָה אַעְבֵיד לִבְרִי:


When you depart from me today, you will find two men by Rachel’s tomb in the territory of Benjamin at Zelzah, and they will say to you, “The asses which you went to seek are found, and now your father has left off the matter of the asses and is anxious about you, saying, ‘What shall I do about my son?’”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:3)


וּתְהַךְ מִתַּמָן וּלְהָלָא וְתֵיתֵי עַד מֵישַׁר תָּבוֹר וְיִשְׁכְּחוּנָךְ תַּמָן תְּלָתָא גוּבְרִין דְסַלְקִין לְמִסְגַד קֳדָם יְיָ בְּבֵית אֵל חַד נָטִיל תְּלָתָא גַדְיָן וְחַד נָטִיל תְּלַת פִּתִּין דִלְחַם וְחַד נָטִיל גַרְבָא דַחֲמָר:


Then you shall go on from there further, and come to the valley of Tabor; three men who are going up to bow down before the LORD at Bethel will meet you there, one carrying three kids, another carrying three loaves of bread, and another carrying a skin of wine.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:4)


וְיִשְׁאֲלוּן לָךְ לִשְׁלָם וְיִתְּנוּן לָךְ תַּרְתֵּין גְרִיצָן דִלְחֵם וּתְקַבֵּל מִידֵיהוֹן:


And they will ask you about your well being and give you two loaves of wreathed bread, which you shall accept from their hand.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:5)


בָּתַר כֵּן תְּהַךְ לְגִבְעַתָא דִי בָה אֲרוֹנָא דַייָ דְתַמָן אִסְטַרְטִינֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וִיהֵי כְמֵיעֲלָךְ תַּמָן לְקַרְתָּא וּתְעָרַע סִיעַת סַפְרַיָא נַחֲתִין מִבֵּית אַסְחָרוּתָא וְקָדָמֵיהוֹן נִבְלִין וְתוּפִּין וְצֶלְצְלִין וְכִנוֹרִין וְאִינוּן מְשַׁבְּחִין:


After that you shall go to the hill where the ark of the LORD is, where the praetors of the Philistines are. And there, as you come to the city, you will meet a company of scribes coming down from the banqueting hall and before them harps, tambourines, cymbals and lyres, and they will be praising.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:6)


וְתִשְׁרֵי עֲלָךְ רוּחַ נְבוּאָה מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּתְשַׁבַּח עִמְהוֹן וְתִשְׁתַּנֵי לִגְבַר אוֹחֲרָן:


Then a prophetic spirit from before the LORD will dwell upon you, and you shall praise with them and be turned into another man.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:7)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יֵיתוּן אָתַיָא הָאִלֵן לָךְ עֲבֵיד לָךְ מָנֵי מַלְכוּתָא אֲרֵי מֵימְרָא דַייָ בְּסַעֲדָךְ:


When these signs will come to you, prepare for yourselves the instruments of kingship, for the speech of the LORD is in your aid.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:8)


וְתֵיחוֹת קֳדָמַי לְגִלְגָלָא וְהָא אֲנָא נָחִית לְוָתָךְ לְאַסָקָא עֲלַוָן וּלְנַכְסָא נִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין תּוֹרִיךְ עַד מֵיתֵי לְוָתָךְ וַאֲחַוֵי לָךְ יַת דְתַעֲבֵּיד:


And you shall go down before me to Gilgal; and behold, I am coming down to you to sacrifice burnt offerings and to slaughter holy slaughterings. Seven days you shall wait, until I come to you and tell you what you shall do.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:9)


וַהֲוָה כַּד אִתְפְּנֵי כִתְפֵיהּ לְמֵיזַל מִלְוַת שְׁמוּאֵל וְשַׁנִי לֵיהּ יְיָ לִבָּא אוֹחֲרָנָא וַאֲתָאָה כָּל אָתַיָא הָאִלֵין בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא:


And when he turned away from Samuel, the LORD gave him another heart. And all these signs came to pass that day.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:10)


וַאֲתוֹ לְתַמָן לְגִבְעֲתָא וְהָא סִיעַת סַפְרַיָא לָקֳדָמוּתֵיהּ וּשְׁרַת עֲלוֹהִי רוּחַ נְבוּאָה מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וְשַׁבַּח בֵּינֵיהוֹן:


When they came there, to the hill, behold, a company of scribes met them; and a prophetic spirit from before the LORD dwelt upon him, and he praised among them.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:11)


וַהֲוָה כָּל דִידַע לֵיהּ מֵאִתְמָלֵי וּמִדְקָמוֹהִי וַחֲזוֹ וְהָא עִם סַפְרַיָא מְשַׁבַּח וַאֲמַר עַמָא גְבַר לְחַבְרֵיהּ מָה דֵין הֲוָה לְבַר קִישׁ הֲאַף שָׁאוּל בְּסַפְרַיָא:


And when all who knew him before saw that he was praising among the scribes, the people said, one to another, “What is this that has come over the son of Kish? Is Saul also among the scribes?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:12)


וַאֲתֵיב גַבְרָא מִתַּמָן וַאֲמַר וּמַן רַבְּהוֹן עַל כֵּן הֲוַת לְמַתְלָא הֲאַף שָׁאוּל בְּסַפְרַיָא:


And a man from there answered, and said, “And who is their master?” Therefore it became a proverb, “Is Saul also among the scribes?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:13)


וּפְסַק מִלְשַׁבָּחָא וְעַל לְבֵית אַסְחֲרוּתָא:


When he had finished praising, he entered the banqueting hall.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:14)


וַאֲמַר אַח אֲבוּהִי דְשָׁאוּל לֵיהּ וּלְעוּלֵימֵיהּ לְאָן אֲזַלְתּוּן וַאֲמַר לְמִבְעֵי יַת אֲתָנַיָא וַחֲזֵינָא אֲרֵי לָא אַשְׁכַּחְנָא וַאֲתֵינָא לְוַת שְׁמוּאֵל:


Saul’s uncle said to him and to his young man, “Where did you go?” And he said, “To seek the asses. And when we saw we would not find them, we went to Samuel.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:15)


וַאֲמַר אַח אֲבוּהִי דְשָׁאוּל חַוֵי כְעַן לִי מָה אֲמַר לְכוֹן שְׁמוּאֵל:


And Saul’s uncle said, “Now, tell me what Samuel said to you.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:16)


וַאֲמַר שָׁאוּל לְאַח אֲבוּהִי חַוָאָה חַוֵי לָנָא אֲרֵי אִשְׁתַּכָּחָא אֲתָנַיָא וְעַל עֵיסַק מַלְכוּתָא לָא חַוֵי לֵיהּ דִי אֲמַר שְׁמוּאֵל:


And Saul said to his uncle, “He told us plainly that the asses had been found.” But about the matter of the kingdom he did not tell him what Samuel had said.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:17)


וּכְנַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל יַת עַמָא לָקֳדָם יְיָ לְמִצְפַּיָא:


Now Samuel gathered the people together before the LORD at Mizpah.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:18)


וַאֲמַר לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כִּדְנַן אָמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲנָא אַסְקֵית יַת יִשְׂרָאֵל מִמִצְרַיִם וְשֵׁיזְבִית יַתְכוֹן מִידָא דְמִצְרָאֵי וּמִיַד כָּל מַלְכְּוָתָא דְדַחֲקִין יַתְכוֹן:


And he said to the sons of Israel, “Thus said the LORD the God of Israel, ‘I brought Israel up out of Egypt, and I delivered you from the hand of the Egyptians and from the hand of all the kingdoms that were oppressing you.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:19)


וְאַתּוּן יוֹמָא דֵין קַצְתּוּן בְּפוּלְחָנָא דֶאֱלָהָכוֹן דְהוּא פָרִיק לְכוֹן מִכָּל בִּישָׁתֵיכוֹן וַעֲקַתְכוֹן וַאֲמַרְתּוּן קֳדָמוֹהִי לֵית אֲנַחְנָא מִתְפָּרְקִין אֱלָהֵין מַלְכָּא תְּמַנֵי עֲלָנָא וּכְעַן אִתְעַתָּדִי קֳדָם יְיָ לְשִׁבְטֵיכוֹן וּלְאַלְפֵיכוֹן:


But you have this day despised the worship of your God, the one who saved you from all your evil and your distress, and you have said before Him, ‘We cannot be saved, unless you appoint a king over us.’ Now, take your stand before the LORD by your tribes and by your thousands.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:20)


וְקָרֵיב שְׁמוּאֵל יַת כָּל שִׁבְטַיָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִתְאֲחַד שִׁבְטָא דְבֵית בִּנְיָמִין:


Then Samuel brought all the tribes of Israel near, and the tribe of the House of Benjamin was singled out.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:21)


וְקָרֵיב יַת שִׁבְטָא דְבִנְיָמִין לְזַרְעֲיָתֵהּ וְאִתְאֲחָדַת זַרְעִית מַטְרִי וְאִתְאֲחַד שָׁאוּל בַּר קִישׁ וּבָעוּהִי וְלָא אִשְׁתְּכַח:


He brought the tribe of the House of Benjamin near by its families, and the family of Matar was singled out. Then Saul the son of Kish was singled out. But when they sought him, he could not be found.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:22)


וּשְׁאִילוּ עוֹד בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ הֲאִית עוֹד הָכָא גַבְרָא וַאֲמַר יְיָ הָא הוּא טְמִיר בְּמָנַיָא:


So they inquired again of the speech of the LORD “Is the man still here?” And the LORD said, “Behold, he is hidden among the vessels.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:23)


וּרְהָטוּ וְדַבְּרוֹהִי מִתַּמָן וְאִתְעֲתַּד בְּגוֹ עַמָא וְרָם מִכָּל עַמָא מִכַּתְפֵיהּ וּלְעֵלָא:


And they ran and led him from there. And when he stood among the people, he was taller than all the people from his shoulder upward.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:24)


וַאֲמַר שְׁמוּאֵל לְכָל עַמָא הַחֲזֵיתוּן דִי אִתְרְעֵי בֵּיהּ יְיָ אֲרֵי לֵית דִכְוָתֵיהּ בְּכָל עַמָא וְיַבִּיבוּ כָל עַמָא וַאֲמַרוּ יַצְלַח מַלְכָּא:


And Samuel said to all the people, “Do you see whom the LORD has chosen? For there is none like him among all the people.” And all the people shouted, “May the king prosper!”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:25)


וּמַלֵיל שְׁמוּאֵל עִם עַמָא יַת נִימוּסָא דְמַלְכָּא וּכְתַב בְּסִפְרָא וְאַצְנַע קֳדָם יְיָ וּשְׁלַח שְׁמוּאֵל יַת כָּל עַמָא גְבַר לְבֵיתֵיהּ:


Then Samuel discussed the royal rights with the people, and he wrote them in a book and deposited it before the LORD Then Samuel sent all the people away, each to his home.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:26)


וְאַף שָׁאוּל אָזַל לְבֵיתֵיהּ לְגִבְעֲתָא וַאֲזָלוּ עִמֵיהּ קְצַת מִן עַמָא דַחֲלֵי חֶטְאָה דְאִתְיְהַב דַחֲלָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ בְּלִבְּהוֹן:


And also Saul went to his home at The Hill, and with him went part of the people, men fearing sin, in whose hearts fear from before the LORD was given.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 10:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/10:27)


וּבְנֵי רִשְׁעָא אֲמַרוּ בְּמָה יִפְרְקִינָנָא דֵין וְשָׁטוֹהִי וְלָא אָתוֹ לְמִשְׁאַל בִּשְׁלָמֵיהּ וַהֲוָה כְּשָׁתֵיק:


But evil men said, “How can this one save us?” And they despised him and did not ask about his well being. But he was like one who is silent.



## Chapter 11

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:1)


וּסְלֵיק נָחָשׁ מַלְכָּא דִבְנֵי עַמוֹן וּשְׁרָא עַל יָבֵשׁ גִלְעָד וַאֲמַרוּ כָּל אֱנָשֵׁי יָבֵשׁ לְנָחָשׁ גְזַר לָנָא קְיָם וְנִפְלְחִינָךְ:


And Nahash, the king of the sons of Ammon, went up and besieged Jabesh gilead; and all the men of Jabesh said to Nahash, “Make a treaty with us, and we will serve you.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:2)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן נָחָשׁ מַלְכָּא דִבְנֵי עַמוֹן בְּהָדָא אֶגְזַר לְכוֹן בְּמֵיקַר לְכוֹן כָּל עֵינָא דְיַמִינָא וַאֲשַׁוִינָה חִיסוּדָא עַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


And Nahash, the king of the sons of Ammon, said to them, “If you do this, I will make a treaty with you in that I gouge out all your right eyes and I put disgrace upon all Israel.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:3)


וַאֲמַרוּ לֵיהּ סָבֵי יָבֵשׁ אוֹרִיךְ לָנָא שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין וְנִשְׁלַח אִזְגַדִין בְּכָל תְּחוּם יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִם לֵית דְפָרִיק יָתָנָא וְנִפּוֹק לְוָתָךְ:


The elders of Jabesh said to him, “Wait for us for seven days that we may send messengers through all the territory of the land of Israel. Then, if there is no one to save us, we will come out to you.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:4)


וַאֲתוֹ אִזְגַדַיָא לְגִבְעֲתָא דְשָׁאוּל וּמַלִילוּ פִּתְגָמַיָא קֳדָם עַמָא וַאֲרִימוּ כָל עַמָא יַת קָלֵיהוֹן וּבְכוֹ:


When the messengers came to The Hill of Saul, they spoke the words before thepeople; and all the people lifted up their voice and wept.

וְהָא שָׁאוּל אָתָא בָּתַר תּוֹרַיָא מִן חַקְלָא וַאֲמַר שְׁאוּל מַה לְעַמָא אֲרֵי בָכָן וְאִשְׁתַּעִיאוּ לֵיהּ יַת פִּתְגָמֵי אֱנָשֵׁי יָבֵשׁ:


Now Saul was coming from the field behind the oxen; and Saul said, “What ails the people, that they are weeping?” So they told him the words of the men of Jabesh.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:6)


וּשְׁרַת רוּחַ גְבוּרָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ עַל שָׁאוּל כַּד שְׁמַע יַת פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִלֵין וּתְקֵיף רוּגְזֵיהּ לַחֲדָא:


And a mighty spirit from before the LORD dwelt upon Saul, when he heard these words, and his anger was very strong.

וּנְסִיב פַּדַן תּוֹרַיָא וְהַדְמֵיהּ וּשְׁלַח בְּכָל תְּחוּם יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּיַד אִזְגַדַיָא לְמֵימַר דְלֵיתוֹהִי נָפֵיק בָּתַר שָׁאוּל וּבָתַר שְׁמוּאֵל כְּדֵין יִתְעֲבֵיד לְתוֹרֵיהּ וּנְפַל זִיעָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ עַל עַמָא וּנְפָקוּ כְּגַבְרָא חָד:


And he took the yoke of oxen and cut them in pieces and sent them throughout allthe territory of Israel by the hand of messengers, saying, “Whoever does not come out after Saul and after Samuel, so shall it be done to his ox!” Then the dread from before the LORD fell upon the people, and they came out as one man.

When he counted them at Bezek, the men of Israel were three hundred thousand and the men of Judah thirty thousand.

וַאֲמַרוּ לְאִזְגַדַיָא דַאֲתוֹ כְּדֵין תֵּימְרוּן לֶאֱנָשׁ יָבֵשׁ גִלְעָד מְחַר תְּהִי לְכוֹן פּוּרְקָנָא בְּמֵיחַם שִׁמְשָׁא וַאֲתוֹ אִזְגַדַיָא וְחַוִיאוּ לֶאֱנָשֵׁי יָבֵשׁ וַחֲדִיאוּ:


And they said to the messengers who had come, “Thus shall you say to the men of Jabesh gilead, ‘Tomorrow, about the heat of the day, you shall experience salvation.’” When the messengers came and told the men of Jabesh, they rejoiced.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/11:10)


וַאֲמַרוּ אֱנָשֵׁי יָבֵשׁ מְחַר נִפּוֹק לְוַתְכוּן וְתַעְבְּדוּן לָנָא כְּכוֹל דְתַקִין בְּעֵינֵיכוֹן:


And the men of Jabesh said, “Tomorrow we will come out to you, and you may do to us whatever seems right in your sight.”

וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹהִי וּמַנִי שָׁאוּל יַת עַמָא תְּלָתָא מַשִׁירְיָן וַעֲלוֹ בְּגוֹ מַשְׁרִיתָא בְּמַטְרַת צַפְרָא וּמְחוֹ יַת בְּנֵי עַמוֹן עַד מֵיחַם יוֹמָא וַהֲווֹ דְאִשְׁתָּאֲרוֹ וְאִתְבַּדָרוּ וְלָא אִשְׁתָּאֲרוּ בְּהוֹן תְּרֵין דְעַרְקִין כַּחֲדָא:


And on the day after it, Saul put the people in three camps; and they came onto the midst of the camp in the morning watch, and struck down the sons of Ammon until the heat of the day. And those who were left were scattered, so that no two of them were left to flee together.

Then the people said to Samuel, “Who is it that said scornfully, ‘Saul is not fit to reign over us.’ Bring the men, that we may kill them.”

But Saul said, “Not a man shall be killed this day, for this day the LORD haswrought salvation in Israel.”

Then Samuel said to the people, “Come, let us go to Gilgal and there renew the kingdom.”

So all the people went to Gilgal, and there they made Saul king before the Lord in Gilgal.



## Chapter 12

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:1)


וַאֲמַר שְׁמוּאֵל לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל הָא קַבְּלֵית לְמֵימְרֵיכוֹן לְכָל דִי אֲמַרְתּוּן לִי וְאַמְלְכֵית עֲלֵיכוֹן מַלְכָּא:


And Samuel said to all Israel, “Behold, I have accepted your speech, all that you have said to me, and have made a king over you.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:2)


וּכְעַן הָא מַלְכָּא מְדַבֵּר בְּרֵישֵׁיכוֹן וַאֲנָא קָשִׁישׁ וְסָבֵית וּבְנַי הָא אִינוּן עִמְכוֹן וַאֲנָא הַלְכֵית קֳדָמֵיכוֹן מִזְעֵירַי עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין:


And now, behold, the king leads you at your head; and I am old and grey, and behold, my sons are with you and I walked before you from my youth until this day.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:3)


הָא עַד דִי אֲנָא קַיָם אַסְהִידוּ בִי קֳדָם יְיָ וְקָדָם מְשִׁיחֵיהּ יַת תּוֹרָא דְמַן דַבָּרִית וַחֲמָרָא דְמַן שְׁחָרִית וְיַת מַן עֲשָׁקִית וְיַת מַן אֲנָסִית וּמִיַד מַן קַבְּלִית מָמוֹן דִשְׁקַר וּכְבֵישִׁית עֵינַי בְּדִינָא מִנֵיהּ וַאֲתֵיב לְכוֹן:


Behold, while I am living, testify against me before the LORD and before his anointed. Whose ox have I led away? And whose ass have I stolen? And whom have I defrauded? Whom have I oppressed? And of whose hand have I accepted money of falsehood to withhold my eye from him in court? And I will restore it to you!”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:4)


וַאֲמַרוּ לָא עֲשַׁקְתָּנָא וְלָא אֲנַסְתָּנָא וְלָא קַבֵּילְתָּא מִיַד אֱנָשׁ מִדָעַם:


And they said, “You have not defrauded us or oppressed us or accepted anything from any man’s hand.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:5)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן סְהֵיד מֵימְרָא דַייָ בְּכוֹן וְסָהֵיד מְשִׁיחֵיהּ יוֹמָא הָדֵין אֲרֵי לָא אַשְׁכַּחְתּוּן בִּידִי מִדָעַם וַאֲמָרוּ סָהִיד:


And he said to them, “The speech of the LORD is witness against you, and his anointed is witness this day, that you have not found anything in my hand.” And they said, “Witness.”

וַאֲמַר שְׁמוּאֵל לְעַמָא יְיָ דַעֲבַד גְבוּרָן עַל יְדֵי מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן וּדְאַסֵיק יַת אֲבָהַתְכוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


And Samuel said to the people, “[It is] the LORD, who did mighty deeds through the hands of Moses and Aaron, and who brought your fathers up out of the land of Egypt.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:7)

So now, stand and let me testify with you before the LORD all the righteousness of the LORD which he performed for you and for your fathers.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:8)


כַּד עַל יַעֲקֹב לְמִצְרָיִם וּזְעִיקוּ אֲבָהַתְכוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ וּשְׁלַח יְיָ יַת מֹשֶׁה וְיַת אַהֲרֹן וְאַפִּיקוּ יַת אֲבָהַתְכוֹן מִמִצְרַיִם וְאוֹתִיבִינוּן בְּאַתְרָא הָדֵין:


When Jacob had gone into Egypt and your fathers cried before the LORD, the LORD sent Moses and Aaron, who brought forth your fathers out of Egypt, and made them dwell in this place.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:9)


וְאִתְנְשִׁיאוּ יַת פּוּלְחָנָא דַייָ אֱלָהָהוֹן וּמְסַר יַתְהוֹן בְּיַד סִיסְרָא רַב חֵילָא דְחָצוֹר וּבִידָא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וּבִידָא מַלְכָּא דְמוֹאָב וְאַגִיחוּ קְרָבָא בְּהוֹן:


But they forgot the worship of the LORD their God; and He surrendered them into the hand of Sisera, commander of the army of Hazor, and into the hand of the Philistines, and into the hand of the king of Moab, and they waged war against them.

וּזְעִיקוּ קֳדָם יְיָ וַאֲמַרוּ חַבְנָא אֲרֵי שְׁבַקְנָא יַת פּוּלְחָנָא דַייָ וּפְלַחְנָא יַת בְּעָלַיָא וְיַת עַשְׁתְּרָתָא וּכְעַן שֵׁיזְבָנָא מִיַד בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָנָא וְנִפְלַח קֳדָמָךְ:


And they cried before the LORD and said, ‘We are guilty, because we have forsaken the worship of the LORD, and have worshipped the Baals and the Ashtaroth. But now, deliver us out of the hand of our enemies, and we will worship Thee.’

וּשְׁלַח יְיָ יַת גִדְעוֹן וְיַת שִׁמְשׁוֹן וְיַת יִפְתָּח וְיַת שְׁמוּאֵל וְשֵׁיזֵיב יַתְכוֹן מִיַד בַעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן מִסְחוֹר סְחוֹר וִיתֵיבְתּוּן לְרוֹחֲצָן:


And the LORD sent Gideon and Samson and Jephthah and Samuel, and delivered you from the hand of your enemies on every side; and you dwelt in safety.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:12)


וַחֲזֵיתוּן אֲרֵי נָחָשׁ מַלְכָּא דִבְנֵי עַמוֹן אָתָא עֲלֵיכוֹן וַאֲמַרְתּוּן לִי לָא אֱלָהֵין מַלְכָּא יִמְלוֹךְ עֲלָנָא וַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן מַלְכְּכוֹן:


And when you saw that Nahash the king of the sons of Ammon came against you, you said to me, ‘No, but a king shall reign over us,’ while the LORD your God was your King.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:13)


וּכְעַן הָא מַלְכָּא דְאִתְרְעִיתוּן דִי שְׁאֶלְתּוּן וְהָא מַנֵי יְיָ עֲלֵיכוֹן מַלְכָּא:


And now, behold the king whom you have chosen, for whom you have asked; and behold, the LORD has appointed a king over you.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:14)


אִם תִּדְחֲלוּ קֳדָם יְיָ וְתִפְלְחוּן קֳדָמוֹהִי וּתְקַבְּלוּן לְמֵימְרֵיהּ וְלָא תְסָרְבוּן עַל מֵימְרָא דַייָ וְתֶהֱווּן אַף אַתּוּן וְאַף מַלְכָּא דִמְלַךְ עֲלֵיכוֹן בָּתַר פּוּלְחָנָא דַייָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


If you shall fear from before the LORD and worship before Him and receive his word and you shall not rebel against the speech of the LORD then both you and the king who reigns over you shall live in worship of the LORD your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:15)


וְאִם לָא תְקַבְּלוּן לְמֵימְרָא דַייָ וּתְסָרְבוּן עַל מֵימְרָא דַייָ וּתְהֵי מְחָתָא דַייָ בְּכוֹן כְּמָא דַהֲוַת בַּאֲבָהַתְכוֹן:


But if you will not accept the speech of the LORD, but rebel against the speech of the LORD then the stroke of the LORD will be on you, as it was on your fathers.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:16)


אַף כְּעַן אִיתְעַתָּדוּ וַחֲזוֹ יַת פִּתְגָמָא רַבָּא הָדֵין דַייָ עָבֵיד לְעֵינֵיכוֹן:


Now again, stand and see this great thing, which the LORD is doing before your eyes.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:17)


הֲלָא חֲצַד חִטִין יוֹמָא דֵין אֲצַלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִתֵּן קָלִין וּמִטְרָא וּדְעוֹ וַחֲזוֹ אֲרֵי בִישַׁתְכוֹן סַגִיאָה דַעֲבַדְתּוּן קֳדָם יְיָ לְמִישְׁאַל לְכוֹן מַלְכָּא:


Is it not wheat harvest today? I will pray before the LORD that He may send thunderclaps and rain; and know and see that your wickedness is great, which you have done beforethe LORD, in asking for yourselves a king.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:18)


וּקְרָא שְׁמוּאֵל קֳדָם יְיָ וִיהַב יְיָ קָלִין וּמִטְרָא בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא וּדְחַל כָּל עַמָא לַחֲדָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּמִפִּתְגָמֵי שְׁמוּאֵל:


So Samuel prayed before the LORD, and the LORD gave thunderclaps and rain that day. And all the people greatly feared before the LORD and (greatly feared) the words of Samuel.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:19)


וַאֲמַרוּ כָּל עַמָא לִשְׁמוּאֵל צַלֵי עַל עַבְדָךְ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וְלָא נְמוּת אֲרֵי אוֹסִיפְנָא עַל כָּל חוֹבָנָא בִּישְׁתָא לְמִשְׁאַל לָנָא מַלְכָּא:


And all the people said to Samuel, “Pray on behalf of your servants before the LORD your God, that we may not die, for we have added to all our guilt the evil to ask for ourselves a king.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:20)


וַאֲמַר שְׁמוּאֵל לְעַמָא לָא תִדְחֲלוּן אַתּוּן גְרַמְתּוּן יַת כָּל בִּישְׁתָּא הָדָא בְּרַם לָא תִסְטוּן מִבָּתַר פּוּלְחָנָא דַייָ וְתִפְלְחוּן קֳדָם יְיָ בְּכָל לִבְּכוֹן:


And Samuel said to the people, “Fear not; you may have brought about all this evil; even so do not turn aside from the worship of the LORD, but worship before the LORD with all your heart.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:21)


וְלָא תִסְטוּן מִבָּתַר פּוּלְחָנֵיהּ וְלָא תִפְלְחוּן לְטַעֲוָתָא דְאִינוּן לְמָא וְלֵית בְּהוֹן הֲנָאָה וְלָא מְשֵׁיזְבִין אֲרֵי לְמָא אִינוּן:


And do not turn aside from his worship and do not worship the idols, which are nothing, and which can neither profit nor deliver, for they are nothing.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:22)


אֲרֵי לָא יַרְחֵיק יְיָ יַת עַמֵיהּ בְּדִיל שְׁמֵיהּ רַבָּא אֲרֵי רַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְמֶעְבַּד יַתְכוֹן קֳדָמוֹהִי לְעָם:


For the LORD will not reject his people, for his great name’s sake, because it has been the will before the LORD to make you a people for himself.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:23)


אַף אֲנָא חַס לִי מִלְמֶחְטֵי קֳדָם יְיָ מִלְאִתְמְנָעָא לְצַלָאָה עֲלֵיכוֹן וְאַלֵיף יַתְכוֹן דִתְהָכוּן בְּאוֹרַח תַּקְנָא וְכַשְׁרָא:


As for me, far be it from me that I should sin before the LORD and cease to pray concerning you. But I will teach you the way, that you may be in the right and the proper way.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 12:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/12:24)


בְּרַם דְחִילוּ מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וְתִפְלְחוּן קֳדָמוֹהִי בִּקְשׁוֹט בְּכָל לִבְּכוֹן אֲרֵי חֲזוֹ יַת דְאַסְגֵי לְמֶעְבַּד עִמְכוֹן:


Only fear before the LORD, and worship him faithfully with all your heart. For consider what great things He has done with you.

וְאִם אַבְאָשָׁא תַּבְאֲשׁוּן עוֹבָדֵיכוֹן אַף אַתּוּן אַף מַלְכְּכוֹן תִּשְׁתֵּיצוּן:


But if you really do wicked deeds, both you and your king shall be destroyed.”



## Chapter 13

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:1)


כְּבַר שְׁנָא דְלֵית בֵּיהּ חוֹבִין כֵּן שָׁאוּל כַּד מְלַךְ וְתַרְתֵּין שְׁנִין מְלַךְ עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


As a one year old child, in whom there is no guilt, was Saul, when he became king; and he reigned two years over Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:2)


וּבְחַר לֵיהּ שָׁאוּל תְּלָתָא אַלְפִין מִיִשְׂרָאֵל וַהֲווֹ עִם שָׁאוּל תְּרֵין אַלְפִין בְּמִכְמָשׁ וּבְטוּר בֵּית אֵל וְאַלְפָא הֲווֹ עִם יוֹנָתָן בְּגִבְעֲתָא דְבֵית בִּנְיָמִין וּשְׁאַר עַמָא שְׁלַח גְבַר לְקִירְוֹהִי:


And Saul chose three thousand of Israel – two thousand were with Saul in Michmas and the hill country of Bethel, and a thousand with Jonathan in The Hill of the House of Benjamin – and the rest of the people he sent away, each man to his own city.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:3)


וּמְחָא יוֹנָתָן יַת אִסְטַרְטִיגֵי פְלִישְׁתָּאֵי דִי בְגִבְעֲתָא וּשְׁמָעוּ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וְשָׁאוּל תָּקַע בְּשׁוֹפָרָא בְּכָל אַרְעָא לְמֵימַר יִשְׁמְעוּן יְהוּדָאֵי:


And Jonathan slew the praetor of the Philistines, who was in The Hill, and the Philistines heard of it. And Saul blew the trumpet throughout all the land, saying, “Let the Judaeans hear.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:4)


וְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל שְׁמָעוּ לְמֵימַר מְחָא שָׁאוּל יַת אִסְטַרְטִיגֵי פְלִישְׁתָּאֵי וְאַף אִתְגְרִי יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וְאִתְכְּנִישׁוּ עַמָא בָּתַר שָׁאוּל לְגִלְגָלָא:


And all Israel heard it said that Saul had slain the praetor of the Philistines, and also that Israel had attacked the Philistines. And the people were gathered after Saul in Gilgal.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:5)


וּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי אִתְכְּנִישׁוּ לְאַגָחָא קְרָבָא בְיִשְׂרָאֵל תְּלָתִין אַלְפִין רְתִיכִין וְשִׁתָּא אַלְפִין פָּרָשִׁין וְעַמָא כְּחָלָא דְעַל כֵּיף יַמָא לִסְגֵי וּסְלִיקוּ וּשְׁרוֹ בְמִכְמַשׁ מַדְנַח בֵּית אָוֶן:


And the Philistines gathered together to wage war with Israel: thirty thousand chariots and six thousand horsemen and people like the sand on the seashore in multitude. They came up and encamped in Michmas, to the east of Beth aven.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/13:6)


וֶאֱנַשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל חֲזוֹ אֲרֵי עָקַת לְהוֹן אֲרֵי אִידְחַק עַמָא וְאִטַמָרוּ עַמָא בִּמְעָרָתָא וּבִמְצָדָתָא וּבִשְׁקִיפֵי כֵיפַיָא וּבִמְעָרַת טִינָרָא וּבְגוּבַיָא:


When the men of Israel saw that they were in distress, because the people were pressed, the people hid themselves in caves and in strongholds and in clefts of rocks and in caves of flints and in pits.

וִיהוֹדָאֵי עֲבָרוּ יַת יַרְדְנָא לְאַרַע גָד וְגִלְעָד וְשָׁאוּל עַד כְּעַן בְּגִלְגָלָא וְכָל עַמָא אִיתְכְּנִישׁוּ בַתְרוֹהִי:


And the Judaeans crossed the Jordan to the land of Gad and Gilead. Saul was in Gilgal until then and all the people had gathered together after him.

וְאוֹרִיךְ שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין לְזִימְנָא דַאֲמַר לֵיהּ שְׁמוּאֵל וְלָא אָתָא שְׁמוּאֵל לְגִלְגָלָא וְאִיתְבַּדַר עַמָא מֵעִילַוֹהִי:


He had waited seven days for the time that Samuel had said, but Samuel had not come to Gilgal and the people were scattering from him.

So Saul said, “Bring the burnt offering and the holy slaughterings near to me.” And he sacrificed the burnt offering.

וַהֲוָה כְּשֵׁיצָיוּתֵיהּ לְאַסָקָא עֲלָתָא וְהָא שְׁמוּאֵל אָתָא וּנְפַק שָׁאוּל לָקֳדָמוּתֵיהּ לְמִשְׁאַל בִּשְׁלָמֵיהּ:

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 13:12

For as the LORD lives who saved Israel, though it be in Jonathan my son, he shall surely be killed.” But there was none among all the people that answered him.

## Chapter 16

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לִשְׁמוּאֵל עַד אֵימָתַי אַתְּ מִתְאַבֵּל אֶל שָׁאוּל וַאֲנָא רְחַקְתֵּיהּ מִלְמֶהֱוֵי מַלְכָּא עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל מְלֵי קַרְנָךְ מִשְׁחָא וְאִיתָא אֶשְׁלְחִינָךְ לְוַת יִשַׁי דְמִבֵּית לֶחֶם אֲרֵי גְלֵי קֳדָמַי בִּבְנוֹהִי כָּשַׁר קֳדָמַי לְמֶהֱוֵי מַלְכָּא:


And the LORD said to Samuel, “How long will you mourn over Saul, while I have removed him from being king over Israel? Fill your horn with oil and go; I send you to Jesse, who is from Bethlehem, for there is revealed before Me among his sons one fit before Me to be the king.”

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 17:1

וְקוּלַס דִנְחַשׁ עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ וְשִׁרְיָן גַלְבִּין הוּא לָבֵישׁ וּמַתְקַל שִׁרְיוֹנָא חַמְשָׁא אַלְפִין תִּקְלֵי נְחָשָׁא:


A helmet of bronze was on his head, and he was dressed with a breastplate with notches, and the weight of the breastplate was five thousand shekels of bronze.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:6)


וּטְרַקְלִילִין דִנְחַשׁ עַל רִגְלוֹהִי וּמְסַחֲפָא דִנְחָשָׁא נָפִיק מִן קוּלְסָא וּמְטַל בֵּין כְּתֵפוֹהִי:


Greaves of bronze upon his legs, and a shield of bronze, coming forth from the helmet, borne between his shoulders.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:7)


וְאָעָא דְמוּרְנִיתֵיהּ כְּאַכְסָן דְגַרְדָאִין וְשִׁנָנָא דְמוּרְנִיתֵיהּ מַתְקַל שִׁית מְאָה תִקְלֵי פַרְזְלָא וְנָטַל תְּרֵיסָא אָזֵיל קֳדָמוֹהִי:


And the wood of his spear was like a weaver’s heddle rod, and the point of his spear weighed six hundred shekels of iron; and his shield bearer went before him.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:8)


וְקָם וְאַכְרֵיז עַל סִדְרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן לְמָה תִפְקוּן לְסַדְרָא קְרָבָא הֲלָא אֲנָא פְלִשְׁתָּאָה וְאַתּוּן עַבְדִין לְשָׁאוּל (אֲנָא הוּא גָלְיָת פְּלִשְׁתְּאָה דְמִן גַת דִקְטָלִית תְּרֵין בְּנֵי עֵלִי כַּהֲנֵי חָפְנִי וּפִינְחָס וּשְׁבֵיתִי יַת אַרוֹן קֳיָמָא דַייָ וְאוֹבֵלִית יָתֵיהּ לְבֵית דָגוֹן טַעֲוָתִי וַהֲוָה תַמָן בְּקִירְוֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי שַׁבְעָא יַרְחִין אַף כָּל קְרָב וּקְרָב דַהֲווֹ לְהוֹן לִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי אֲנָא נָפֵיק בְּרֵישׁ חֵילָא וּנְצַחְנָא בִקְרָבָא וּרְמִינָא קְטִילִין כְּעַפְרָא דְאַרְעָא וְעַד כְּעַן לָא אַכְשָׁרוּ יָתִי פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי לְמֶהֱוֵי רַב אַלְפָא עִלַוֵיהוֹן וְאַתּוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מַה גְבוּרָה עֲבַד לְכוֹן שָׁאוּל בַּר קִישׁ דְמִן גִבְעֲתָא דְמַנִיתוּן יָתֵיהּ מַלְכָּא עִלַוֵיכוֹן אִם גְבַר גִבָּר הוּא יֵיחוֹת וְיַעְבֵּיד קְרָבָא עִמִי וְאִם גְבַר חַלָשׁ הוּא) בְּחַרוּ לְכוֹן גַבְרָא וְיֵחוֹת לְוָתִי:


And he arose and shouted against the ranks of Israel, and said to them, “Why have you come out to draw up for battle? Am I not the Philistine, and are you not servants of Saul? Choose a man for yourselves, and let him come down to me.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 17:9

אִם יִכּוּל לַאֲגָחָא קְרָבָא עִמִי וְיִקְטְלִינַנִי וּנְהֵי לְכוֹן לְעַבְדִין וְאִם אֲנָא אִכּוּל לֵיהּ וְאַקְטִלִינֵיהּ וּתְהוֹן לָנָא לְעַבְדִין וְתִפְלְחוּן יָתָנָא:


If he is able to fight with me and kill me, then we will be your servants; but if I prevail against him, and kill him, then you shall be our servants and serve us.”

וּשְׁמַע שָׁאוּל וְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל יַת פִּתְגָמֵי פְּלִשְׁתָּאָה הָאִלֵין וְאִתְבָּרוּ וּדְחִילוּ לַחֲדָא:

וּשְׁמַע אֱלִיאָב אֲחוּהִי רַבָּא בְּמַלָלוּתֵיהּ עִם גַבְרַיָא וּתְקֵיף רוּגְזָא דֶאֱלִיאָב בְּדָוִד וַאֲמַר לְמָה דְנַן נְחַתְתָּא וְעַל מַן רְטַשְׁתָּא זְעֵיר עָנָא הָאִינוּן בְּמַדְבְּרָא אֲנָא יְדַעְנָא יַת בַּקְרְנוּתָךְ וְיַת בִּישׁוּת לִבָּךְ אֲרֵי בְּדִיל לְמֶחְזֵי עָבְדֵי קְרָבָא נְחַתְתָּא:


Now Eliab his eldest brother heard when he spoke to the men, and Eliab’s anger was kindled against David, and he said, “Why have you come down? And with whom have you left those few sheep in the wilderness? I know your exuberance, and the evil of your heart; for you have come down to see the wagers of war.”

And David said, “What have I done now? Was it not but a word?”

וְקָמוּ אֱנָשֵׁי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהוּדָה וְיַבִּיבוּ וּרְדָפוּ יַת פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי עַד מַעֲלָנָא דְגַיְא וְעַד תַּרְעֵי עֶקְרוֹן וּנְפַלוּ קָטוֹלֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי בְּאוֹרַח שַׁעֲרַיִם וְעַד גַת וְעַד עֶקְרוֹן:


And the men of Israel and Judah rose and shouted and pursued the Philistines as far as the entrance of the valley and the gates of Ekron, so that the Philistines fell wounded on the way of Sha ararim, as far as Gath and Ekron.

And the sons of Israel came back from pursuing the Philistines, and they plundered their camp.

When Saul saw David go forth against the Philistine, he said to Abner, the commander of the army, “Abner, whose son is the young man?” And Abner said, “By your own life, O king, I do not know.”

And Saul said to him, “Whose son are you, young man?” And David said, “I am the son of your servant Jesse, who is from Bethlehem.”

When he had finished speaking with Saul, the soul of Jonathan was tied in love to David’s soul, and Jonathan loved him as his own soul.

And Saul took him that day, and would not let him go, to return to his father’s house.

And Jonathan and David made a covenant, because he loved him as his own soul.

And Jonathan sent his robe that was upon him, and gave it to David, and his clothes, and even his sword and his bow and his girdle.

## Chapter 19

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 19:1

And Saul spoke with Jonathan his son and with all his servants to kill David, but Jonathan, son of Saul, delighted much in David.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:2)

And Jonathan told David, saying, “Saul my father seeks to kill you. And now, take heed to yourself in the morning, and keep hidden in a secret place.

And I will go out and stand in my father’s presence in the field where you are, and I will speak to my father good things about you. Then I will see whatever [happens] and I will tell you.”

And Jonathan spoke good things about David before Saul his father, and said to him, “Let not the king sin against his servant, against David, because he has not sinned against you and because his deeds have been very good for you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:5)


וּמְסַר יַת נַפְשֵׁיהּ כְּעַל גַב לְאִתְקְטָלָא וּקְטַל יַת פְלִשְׁתָּאָה וַעֲבַד יְיָ פּוּרְקָנָא רַבָּא לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל חֲזֵיתָא וַחֲדֵיתָא וּלְמָא תִּתְחַיֵב בְּדַם זַכַּאי לְמִקְטַל יַת דָוִד מַגָן:


For he surrendered his life, as if it were on the back [of his hand], to be possibly killed, as he killed the Philistine, and the LORD wrought a great salvation for all Israel. You saw it and you rejoiced. Why then would you become guilty against innocent blood by killing David without cause?”

וַאֲמַר שָׁאוּל לְמִיכַל לְמָא כְּדֵין שַׁקַרְתְּ בִּי וְשַׁלַחְתְּ יַת בְּעֵיל דְבָבִי וְאִשְׁתֵּיזִיב וַאֲמַרַת מִיכַל לְשָׁאוּל הוּא אֲמַר לִי שַׁלְחֵנִי לְמָה אֶקְטְלִנָךְ:


Saul said to Michal, “Why have you deceived me thus, and let my enemy go, so that he has escaped?” And Michal said to Saul, “He said to me, ‘Let me go. Why should I kill you?’”

וְדָוִד עֲרַק וְאִשְׁתֵּיזֵיב וַאֲתָא לְוַת שְׁמוּאֵל לְרָמָתָא וְחַוֵי לֵיהּ יַת כָּל דִי עֲבַד לֵיהּ שָׁאוּל וַאֲזַל הוּא וּשְׁמוּאֵל וִיתִיבוּ בְּבֵית אוּלְפָנָא:


Now David fled and escaped, and he came to Samuel at Ramah, and told him all that Saul had done to him. And he and Samuel went and dwelt in the Talmud School.

וְאִתְחַוָא לְשָׁאוּל לְמֵימַר הָא דָוִד בְּבֵית אוּלְפָנָא בְּרָמָתָא:


And it was told Saul, saying, “Behold, David is in the Talmud School in Ramah.”

Then Saul sent messengers to take David. And when they saw the company of scribes praising, and Samuel standing as teacher over them, a prophetic spirit from before the LORD dwelt on them, and they also praised.

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 20:1

Then David fled from the Talmud School in Ramah, and came and said before Jonathan, “What have I done? What is my guilt? And what is my sin before your father, that he seeks to kill me?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/20:2)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ חַס לָא תְמוּת הָא לָא יַעְבֵּיד אַבָּא פִּתְגַם רַב אוֹ פִּתְגַם זְעֵיר וְלָא יְחַוֵי לִי וּמָא דֵין כַסֵי אַבָּא מִנִי יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין לֵית דָא קַיָמָא:


And he said to him, “Far be it! You shall not die. Behold, my father does nothing either great or small without telling it to me; and why should my father hide this from me? This is not lasting.”

But David swore, “Your father knows well that I have found favour in your sight. And he says, ‘Let not Jonathan know this, lest he be sorrowed.’ And now, as the LORD is the existing One, and by your own soul, there is the like of one step between me and death.”

And Jonathan said to David, “Whatever the delight of your soul is, I will do for you.”

David said to Jonathan, “Behold, tomorrow is the new moon, and I should surely turn back to eat with the king. But let me go, that I may hide myself in the field till the time of the evening of the third day.

Then came David to Nob to Ahimelech the priest; and Ahimelech came to meet David terrified and said to him, “Why are you alone, and no man with you?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/21:3)


וַאֲמַר דָוִד לַאֲחִימֶלֶךְ כַּהֲנָא מַלְכָּא פַּקְדַנִי פִּתְגָמָא וַאֲמַר לִי אֱנַשׁ לָא יֵדַע מִדַעַם יַת פִּתְגָמָא דַאֲנָא שְׁלַח לָךְ וּדְפַקִדְתָּךְ וְיַת עוּלֵימַיָא שְׁלָחִית קֳדָמַי לַאֲתַר כַּסִי וְטָמִיר:


And David said to Ahimelech the priest, “The king has charged me with a matter and said to me, ‘Let no man know anything of the matter about which I send you and with which I have charged you.’ So I have sent ahead the young men to a hidden and concealed place.

Now then, what have you at hand? Give me five loaves of bread or whatever is found.”

וַאֲתֵיב כַּהֲנָא יַת דָוִד וַאֲמַר לֵית לְחֵם חוֹלִין לִתְחוֹת יְדָי אֱלָהֵין לַחְמָא דְקֻדְשָׁא אִית בְּרַם אִם נָטְרִין עוּלֵימַיָא לְחוֹד מִסוֹאֲבַת אִתְּתָא:


And the priest answered David and said, “I have no profane bread at hand, but there is holy bread; if only the young men have kept themselves from the uncleanness of women.”

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/21:6)


וַאֲתֵיב דָוִד יַת כַּהֲנָא וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ בְּקֻשְׁטָא אִתְּתָא מְרַחֲקָא לָנָא כְּמֵאִתְמָלֵי וּמִדְקֳדָמוֹהִי בְּמִפְקִי וַהֲוָה מָנֵיהוֹן לְעוּלֵימַיָא דְכָן לְקֻדְשָׁא וְהוּא בְּאוֹרַח חוֹלָא הֲוָה אָזֵיל וְיוֹמָא דֵין הֲוָה מִתְאֲכֵיל וְלָא מִתְפְּסַל בְּמָנָא:


And David answered the priest and said to him, “In truth, women are removed from us as always before when I go out. The vessels of the young men are clean for holiness. And it is not going in a profane journey and today it is eaten and not becoming invalid by the vessel.”

So the priest gave him the holy [bread], for there was no common bread there, but only the bread of the Presence, which had been removed from before the LORD to be replaced by hot bread on the day it is taken away.

And the priest said, “The sword of Goliath the Philistine, whom you killed in the Valley of the Terebinth, behold it is here wrapped in a cloak.” After he inquired of the ephod for him, “If you want to take that, take it, for there is none other than that here.” And David said, “There is none like that, give it to me.”

And David went from there and escaped to the cave of Adullam; and when his brothers and all his father’s house heard it, they went down there to him.

And every man troubled of spirit and every man who had a creditor and every man bitter of soul, gathered to him. And he became commander over them. And there were with him about four hundred men.

And David went from there to Mizpah of Moab; and he said to the king of Moab, “Let my father and my mother come out now [to stay] with you, till I know what the LORD will do for me.”

And Saul said to his servants who stood about him, “Hear now, sons of the tribe of the House of Benjamin. Will the son of Jesse give fields and vineyards also to all of you? Will he appoint all of you commanders of thousands and commanders of hundreds?

For all of you have rebelled against me. There was no one who told me, when my son made a covenant with the son of Jesse. And there was no one from you who felt sorry for him and told me that my son stirred up my servant against me to lie in wait, as at this day.”

וַאֲמַר דָוִד הַסְבִירִין לְמִמְסְרִי יַתְבֵי קְעִילָה יָתִי וְיַת גַבְרַי בִּידָא דְשָׁאוּל וַאֲמַר יְיָ סְבִירִין לְמִמְסַר:


Then David said, “Are the inhabitants of Keilah planning to surrender me and my men into the hand of Saul?” And the LORD said, “They are planning to surrender you.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:13)


וְקָם דָוִד וְגַבְרוֹהִי כְּשִׁית מְאָה גַבְרָא וּנְפַקוּ מִקְעִילָה וְהַלִיכוּ בַּאֲתַר דְכָשַׁר לְהַלָכָא וּלְשָׁאוּל אִתְחַוָא אֲרֵי אִשְׁתֵּיזֵיב דָוִד מִקְעִילָה וְאִתְמְנַע מִלְמִפַּק:


Then David and his men – about six hundred men – arose and departed from Keilah, and they went to every place that was fit to go to. When Saul was told that David had escaped from Keilah, he withdrew from going out.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:14)


וִיתֵיב דָוִד בְּמַדְבְּרָא בִּמְצַדְתָּא וִיתֵיב בְּטוּרָא בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְזִיף וּבְעוֹהִי שָׁאוּל כָּל יוֹמַיָא וְלָא מַסְרֵיהּ יְיָ בִּידֵיהּ:


And David dwelt in the strongholds in the desert, in the hill country of the Desert of Ziph. And Saul sought him all the days, but the LORD did not surrender him into his hand.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:15)


וַחֲזָא דָוִד אֲרֵי נְפַק שָׁאוּל לְמִקְטְלֵיהּ וְדָוִד בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְזִיף בְּחוּרְשָׁא:


And David saw that Saul had come out to seek to kill him. David was in the Desert of Ziph, at The Wood.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:16)


וְקָם יְהוֹנָתָן בַּר שָׁאוּל וַאֲזַל לְוַת דָוִד לְחוּרְשָׁא וּתְקֵיף יַת יְדֵיהּ בְּמֵימְרָא דַיָי:


And Jonathan, Saul’s son, rose and went to David at The Wood, and strengthened his hand in the speech of the LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:17)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ לָא תִדְחַל אֲרֵי לָא תַשְׁכְּחִינָךְ יְדָא דְשָׁאוּל אַבָּא וְאַתְּ תִּמְלוֹךְ עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲנָא אֱהֵי לָךְ לְתִנְיָן וְאַף שָׁאוּל אַבָּא יְדַע כֵּן:


And he said to him, “Fear not; for the hand of Saul my father shall not find you. You shall be king over Israel, and I shall be next to you. Saul my father also knows this.”

וּגְזָרוּ תַרְוֵיהוֹן קְיָם קֳדָם יְיָ וִיתֵיב דָוִד בַּחוֹרְשָׁא וִיהוֹנָתָן אֲזַל לְבֵיתֵיהּ:


And the two of them made a covenant before the LORD. And David dwelt in The Wood, while Jonathan went home.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:19)


וּסְלִיקוּ אֱנָשֵׁי זִיף לְוַת שָׁאוּל לְגִבְעֲתָא לְמֵימָר הֲלָא דָוִד מִטַמָר עִמָנָא בַמְצַדְתָּא בַּחוּרְשָׁא בְּגִבְעַת חֲכִילָה דְמִדָרוֹם לִישִׁימוֹן:


Then the men of Ziph went up to Saul at The Hill, saying, “Does not David hide among us in the strongholds of The Wood, on the hill of Hachilah, which is south of Jeshimon?


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 23:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:20)

And now, come down according to all your heart’s desire, O king, and we shall surrender him into the king’s hand.”

וַאֲמַר שָׁאוּל בְּרִיכָן אַתּוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֲרֵי חַסְתּוּן עֲלָי:


And Saul said, “Blessed be you before the LORD, for you have had compassion on me.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 23:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:22)


אֱזִילוּ כְעַן אַתְקִינוּ עוֹד וּדְעוֹ וַחֲזוֹ יַת אַתְרֵיה דְשָׁרֵי בֵיהּ מַן חֲזָהִי תַמָן אֲרֵי אָמְרִין לִי דְעָרִים וְנָפִיק הוּא:


Go now and make more sure. Know and see the place where he dwells. Who saw him there? For they say that he is cunning and moving.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 23:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:23)


וַחֲזוֹ וּדְעוֹ מִכָּל מַטְמוֹרַיָתָא דְאִטְמַר תַּמָן וּתְתוּבוּן לְוָתִי בִּקְשׁוֹט וְאֵהַךְ עִמְכוֹן וִיהֵי אִם אִיתוֹהִי בְאַרְעָא וְאֶבְלוֹשׁ יָתֵיה בְּכָל אַלְפַיָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה:


See then and learn to know any of the hiding places where he hides, and return to me with truth. Then I will go with you, and if he is in the land, I will search him out among all the thousands of Judah.”

And they arose and went to Ziph, ahead of Saul. Now David and his men were in the Desert of Maon, in the valley to the south of Jeshimon.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 23:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:25)


וַאֲזַל שָׁאוּל וְגַבְרוֹהִי לְמִבְעֵי וְחַוִיאוּ לְדָוִד וּנְחַת לְכֵיפָא וִיתֵיב בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְמָעוֹן וּשְׁמַע שָׁאוּל וּרְדַף בָּתַר דָוִד בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְמָעוֹן:


When Saul and his men went to seek him, David was told and he went down to the rock and dwelt in the Desert of Maon. And when Saul heard that, he pursued after David in the Desert of Maon.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 23:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/23:26)


וַאֲזַל שָׁאוּל מִסְטַר טוּרָא מִכָּא וְדָוִד וְגַבְרוֹהִי מִסְטַר טוּרָא מִכָּא וַהֲוָה דָוִד מִתְבְּעֵי לְמֵיזַל מִן קֳדָם שָׁאוּל וְשָׁאוּל וְגַבְרוֹהִי כָּמְנִין עַל דָוִד וְעַל גַבְרוֹהִי לְמֵחַדְהוֹן:


Saul went on one side of the mountain and David and his men on the other side of the mountain. And David was anxious to get away from Saul, as Saul and his men were lying in wait against David and against his men to capture them.

Then a messenger came to Saul, saying, “Make haste and come, for the Philistines have marched out against the land!”

So Saul returned from pursuing after David, and went against the Philistines. Therefore they called the place Rock of Divisions, the place where the heart of the king was divided to go here or there.



## Chapter 24

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:1)


וּסְלֵיק דָוִד מִתַּמָן וִיתֵב בִּמְצַדְתָּא עֵין גֶדִי:


And David went up from there and dwelt in the stronghold of En gedi.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:2)


וַהֲוָה כַּד תַּב שָׁאוּל מִבָתַר פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וְחַוִיאוּ לֵיה לְמֵימַר הָא דָוִד בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְעֵין גֶדִי:


When Saul returned from pursuing after the Philistines, they told him, “Behold, David is in the Desert of En gedi.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:3)


וּדְבַר שָׁאוּל תְּלָתָא אַלְפִין גְבַר עוּלֵים מִכָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲזַל לְמִבְעֵי יַת דָוִד וְגַבְרוֹהִי עַל אַפֵּי שְׁקִיפֵי כֵיפַיָא:


Then Saul took three thousand young men from all Israel and went to seek David and his men superficially in the clefts of the rocks.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:4)


וַאֲתָא לְחַטְרֵי עָאנָא דְעַל אוֹרְחָא וְתַמָן מְעַרְתָּא וְעַל שָׁאוּל לְמֶעְבַּד צוֹרְכֵיהּ וְדָוִד וְגַבְרוֹהִי בִסְיָפֵי מְעַרְתָּא יָתְבִין:


And he came to the sheepfolds that were by the road, where there was a cave. And Saul went in to relieve himself, while David and his men were dwelling in the innermost parts of the cave.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:5)


וַאֲמַרוּ גַבְרֵי דָוִד לֵיהּ הָא יוֹמָא דַאֲמַר יְיָ לָךְ הָא אֲנָא מְסַר יַת בְּעֵיל דְבָבָךְ בִּידָךְ וְתַעֲבֵּיד לֵיהּ כְּמָא דְתַקֵן בְּעֵינָךְ וְקָם דָוִד וּפְסַק יַת כְּנַף מְעִילָא דִלְשָׁאוּל בְּרָז:


And the men of David said to him, “Here is the day of which the LORD said to you, ‘Behold, I will surrender your enemy into your hand, and you shall do to him as it shall be right in your sight.’” Then David arose and stealthily cut off the hem of Saul’s robe.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:6)


וַהֲוָה בָתַר כֵּן וְחָשׁ דָוִד בְּלִבֵּיהּ עַל דִי פְסַק יַת כַּנְפָא דִלְשָׁאוּל:


And afterward David was troubled in his heart, because he had cut off Saul’s hem.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:7)


וַאֲמַר לְגַבְרוֹהִי חָס לִי מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אִם אַעְבֵּיד יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין לְרִבּוֹנִי לִמְשִׁיחָא דַייָ לְאוֹשָׁטָא יְדִי בֵּיהּ אֲרֵי מְשִׁיחָא דַייָ הוּא:


And he said to his men, “Far be it from me – from before the LORD – that I should do this thing to my lord, the LORD’s anointed, to stretch out my hand against him, for he is the LORD’s anointed.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:8)


וּפַיֵס דָוִד יַת גַבְרוֹהִי בְּפִתְגָמַיָא וְלָא שַׁבְקִינוּן לְמֵיקַם עַל שָׁאוּל וְשָׁאוּל קָם מִמְעַרְתָּא וַאֲזַל בְּאוֹרְחָא:


So David calmed down his men with these words, and did not permit them to attack Saul. And Saul rose up from the cave and went upon the road.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:9)


וְקָם דָוִד בָּתַר כֵּן וּנְפַק מִן מְעַרְתָּא וּקְרָא בָּתַר שָׁאוּל לְמֵימָר רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא וְאִסְתְּכֵי שָׁאוּל לַאֲחוֹרוֹהִי וּכְרַע דָוִד עַל אַפּוֹהִי עַל אַרְעָא וּסְגִיד:


Afterward David also arose and went out of the cave. He called after Saul, saying, “My lord the king!” And when Saul looked behind him, David knelt down with his face to the ground and he bowed down.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:10)


וַאֲמַר דָוִד לְשָׁאוּל לְמָא אַתְּ שָׁמַע יַת פִּתְגָמֵי בְנֵי אֱנָשָׁא דְאָמְרִין הָא דָוִד כָּמֵין בִּישְׁתָּךְ:


And David said to Saul, “Why are you listening to the words of the sons of man who are saying, ‘David is lying in wait for your ruin’?


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:11)


הָא יוֹמָא דֵין חֲזָאָה עֵינָךְ יַת דְמַסְרָךְ יְיָ יוֹמָא דֵין בִּידִי בִּמְעַרְתָּא וַאֲמַרוּ אוֹחֲרָנִין לְמִקְטְלָךְ וְחָסִית עֲלָךְ וַאֲמַרִית לָא אוֹשִׁיט יְדִי בְּרִבּוֹנִי אֲרֵי מְשִׁיחָא דַייָ הוּא:


Behold, this day your eyes are seeing how the LORD surrendered you today into my hand in the cave. And others said to kill you, but I spared you and I said, ‘I will not stretch out my hand against my lord, for he is the LORD’s anointed.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:12)


וְרִבּוֹנִי אִסְתַּכַּל אַף חֲזֵי יַת כְּנַף מְעִילָךְ בִּידִי אֲרֵי בְּמִפְסְקִי יַת כְּנַף מְעִילָךְ וְלָא קְטַלְתִּיךְ דַע וַחֲזוֹ אֲרֵי לֵית בִּידִי חוֹב וְסוּרְחַן וְלָא חָטִית לָךְ וְאַתְּ כָּמִין עַל נַפְשִׁי לְמִקְטְלָהּ:


Look, my lord! Yea, see the hem of your robe in my hand! For by the fact that I cut off the hem of your robe and did not kill you, you may know and see that there is neither guilt nor transgression in my hand. I have not sinned against you, though you are lying in wait against my life in order to kill me.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:13)


יְדִין יְיָ בֵּינָא וּבֵינָךְ וְיִתְפְּרַע לִי יְיָ מִנָךְ וִידִי לָא תְהִי בָךְ:


May the LORD judge between me and you. May the LORD avenge me on you. But my hand shall not be against you.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:14)


כְּמָא דְאָמְרִין מַתְלָא דְמִלְקָדְמִין מֵרַשִׁיעִין תִּפּוֹק חוֹבַתְהוֹן וִידִי לָא תְהֵי בָךְ:


Just as they say the proverb of old times, ‘Out of the wicked comes forth their guilt,’ my hand shall not be against you.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:15)


בָּתַר מַן נְפַק מַלְכָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל בָּתַר מַן אַתְּ רָדִיף בָּתַר חֲלַשׁ חָד בָּתַר הֶדְיוֹט חָד:


After whom has Israel’s king come out? After whom do you pursue? After one weak person! After one common man!


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:16)


וִיהֵי יְיָ לְדַיָין וְיַעְבֵּד דִין בֵּינָא וּבֵינָךְ וּגְלֵי קֳדָמוֹהִי וִידוּן דִינִי וְיִתְפְּרַע עוּלְבָּנִי מִמָךְ:


May the Lord be judge and make judgment between me and you! May it be revealed before Him that He judges my case and revenge my humiliation on you!


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:17)


וַהֲוָה כַּד שֵׁיצֵי דָוִד לְמַלָלָא יַת פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִלֵין עִם שָׁאוּל וַאֲמַר שָׁאוּל הֲקָלָךְ דֵין בְּרִי דָוִד וַאֲרֵים שָׁאוּל קָלֵיהּ וּבְכָא:


When David had finished speaking these words to Saul, Saul said, “Is this your voice, my son David?” And Saul lifted up his voice and wept.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:18)


וַאֲמַר לְדָוִד זַכַּאי אַתְּ מִנִי אֲרֵי אַתְּ גְמַלְתַּנִי טַבְתָּא וַאֲנָא גְמַלְתָּךְ בִּישְׁתָא:


He said to David, “You are more innocent than I, for you have repaid me good, whereas I have repaid you evil.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:19)


וְאַתְּ חֲוֵיתָא יוֹמָא דֵין יַת דַעֲבַדְתָּ עִמִי טִיבוּ וְיַת דְמַסְרַנִי יְיָ בִּידָךְ וְלָא קְטַלְתָּנִי:


And you have told today how well you have dealt with me, in that you did not kill me when the LORD surrendered you into your hand.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:20)


וַאֲרֵי יַשְׁכַּח גְבַר יַת בְּעֵיל דְבָבֵיהּ וְיִשְׁלְחִנֵיהּ בְּאוֹרַח תַּקָנָא וַיָי יְשַׁלְמִנָךְ טַבְתָא חֲלַף יוֹמָא דֵין דַעֲבַדְתָּא לִי:


For if a man finds his enemy, will he let him go on a right road? So may the LORD reward you with good for what you have done to me this day.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:21)


וּכְעַן הָא יָדַעְנָא אֲרֵי מִמְלַךְ תִּמְלַךְ וְתִתְקַיֵים בִּידָךְ מַלְכוּתָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


And now, behold, I know that you shall surely be king and that the kingdom of Israel shall be established in your hand.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 24:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/24:22)


וּכְעַן קַיֵים לִי בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ דְלָא תְשֵׁיצֵי יַת בְּנַי בַּתְרָי וְלָא תִפְסוֹק יַת שְׁמִי מִבֵּית אַבָּא:


Now swear to me by the speech of the LORD, that you will not destroy my seed after me and that you will not destroy my name out of my father’s house.”

וְקַיֵם דָוִד לְשָׁאוּל וַאֲזַל שָׁאוּל לְבֵיתֵיהּ וְדָוִד וְגַבְרוֹהִי סְלִיקוּ לִמְצַדְתָּא:


And David swore this to Saul. Then Saul went home, but David and his men went up to the strongholds.



## Chapter 25

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:1)


וּמִית שְׁמוּאֵל וְאִתְכְּנִישׁוּ כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וּסְפָדוּ עֲלוֹהִי וּקְבָרוּהִי בְּבֵיתֵיהּ בְּרָמָה וְקָם דָוִד וּנְחַת לְמַדְבְּרָא דְפָארָן:


Now Samuel died and all Israel gathered and mourned over him. And they buried him in his house at Ramah. Then David rose and went down to the Desert of Paran.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:2)


וְגַבְרָא בְמָעוֹן וְנִכְסוֹהִי בְכַרְמְלָא וְגַבְרָא רַב לַחֲדָא וְלֵיהּ עָאן תְּלָתָא אַלְפִין וְאֶלֶף עִזִין וַהֲוָה בְּמֵיגַז יַת עָנֵיהּ בְּכַרְמְלָא:


And there was a man in Maon, whose herds were in Carmel. The man was very rich, he had three thousand sheep and a thousand goats. And he was engaged in shearing his sheep in Carmel.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:3)


וְשׁוּם גַבְרָא נָבָל וְשׁוּם אִתְּתֵיהּ אֲבִיגָיִל וְאִתְּתָא חֲכִּימַת מַדַע וְשַׁפִּירַת רֵיווּ וְגַבְרָא קָשֵׁי וּבִישׁ עוֹבְדִין וְהוּא מִדְבֵית כָּלֵב:


Now the name of the man was Nabal and the name of his wife Abigail. The woman was wise in knowledge and beautiful of appearance, but the man was harsh and ill behaved. And he was from the House of Caleb.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:4)


וּשְׁמַע דָוִד בְּמַדְבְּרָא אֲרֵי גָזֵיז נָבָל יַת עָנֵיהּ:


David heard in the desert that Nabal was shearing his sheep.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:5)


וּשְׁלַח דָוִד עַשְׂרָא עוּלֵימִין וַאֲמַר דָוִד לְעוּלֵימַיָא סַקוּ לְכַרְמְלָא וּתְהָכוּן לְוַת נָבָל וְתִשְׁאֲלוּן לֵיהּ בִּשְׁמִי לִשְׁלָם:


So David sent ten young men and David said to the young men, “Go up to Carmel and go to Nabal and ask him concerning his well being in my name.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:6)


וְתֵימְרוּן כְּדֵין לְחַיָךְ וְאַתְּ שְׁלָם וּבֵיתָךְ שְׁלָם וְכָל דִילָךְ שְׁלָם:


And you shall say, ‘Thus to your life: peace be to you, and peace be to your house, and peace be to all that you have.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:7)


וּכְעַן שְׁמָעִית אֲרֵי גוֹזְזִין לָךְ כְּעַן רָעַיָא דִילָךְ הֲווֹ עִמָנָא לָא אַנְזֵיקִינוּנוּן וְלָא שְׁגָא לְהוֹן מִדַעַם כָּל יוֹמִין דַהֲווֹ בְּכַרְמְלָא:


And now, I hear that you have shearers. Now your shepherds have been with us and we did them no harm and they missed not anything, all the days that they were in Carmel.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:8)


שְׁאַל יַת עוּלֵמָךְ וִיחַוּן לָךְ וְיִשְׁכְּחוּן עוּלֵימַיָא רַחֲמִין בְּעֵינָךְ אֲרֵי עַל יוֹם טַב אֲתֵינָא הַב כְּעַן יַת דְתַשְׁכַּח יְדָךְ לְעַבְדָךְ וְלִבְרָךְ לְדָוִד:


Ask your young men and they will tell you. And let my young men find favour in your sight, for we come on a festive day. Give now what you have at hand to your servants and to your son, to David.’”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:9)


וַאֲתוֹ עוּלֵימֵי דָוִד וּמַלִילוּ עִם נָבָל כְּכָל פִתְגָמַיָא הָאִלֵין בִּשְׁמָא דְדָוִד וּפְסָקוּ:


When David’s young men came, they spoke according to all these words with Nabal in the name of David. Then they ceased.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:10)


וַאֲתֵיב נָבָל יַת עַבְדֵי דָוִד וַאֲמַר מַן דָוִד וּמַן בַּר יִשַׁי יוֹמָא דֵין דְסַגִיאִין עַבְדַיָא דְעָרְקִין וּדְמִטַמְרִין גְבַר מִן קֳדָם רִבּוֹנֵיהּ:


And Nabal answered David’s servants, “Who is David? And who is the son of Jesse? There are many servants nowadays who are fleeing and hiding from their master.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:11)


וְאֶסַב יַת מֵיכְלֵי וְיַת מִשְׁתִּי וְיַת שֵׁירוּתִי דְאַתְקֵינִית לְגָזְזַי וְאֶתֵּן לְגַבְרַיָא דְלָא יְדַעַת אֵי מִנָן אִנוּן:


And shall I take my food and my drink and my meal that I have prepared for my shearers, and give it to men who come from I do not know where?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:12)


וְאִתְחַזָרוּ עוּלֵמֵי דָוִד לְאוֹרְחַתְהוֹן וְתָבוּ וַאֲתוֹ וְחַוִיאוּ לֵיהּ כְּכָל פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִלֵין:


So David’s young men turned away and returned. And when they came, they told David according to all these words.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 25:13

And Abigail came to Nabal, and behold, he was holding a feast in his house like a feast of kings. And Nabal’s heart was merry within him and he was exceedingly drunk. So she did not tell him anything, much or little, until the morning light.

When David heard that Nabal was dead, he said, “Blessed be the LORD who administered my shameful lawsuit against Nabal and has restrained his servant from evil. And the LORD has returned Nabal’s evil upon his own head.” Then David sent and asked for Abigail to take her for a wife.

This thing that you have done is not right. As the LORD is the existing One, you are men deserving to die, because you have not kept watch over your lord, over the LORD’s anointed. And now, see where the king’s spear is, and the vessel of water that was at his head.

And Saul recognized David’s voice and said, “Is this your voice, my son David?” And David said, “It is my voice, my lord, O king.”

And he said, “Why does my lord pursue after his servant? For what have I done? There is no guilt on my hands!

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:19)

And now, let my lord the king hear the words of his servant. If it is from before the LORD that you are incited against me, let your offering be accepted with delight. But if it is from the sons of men, cursed are they before the LORD, for they have driven me today from dwelling on the heritage of the LORD’s people, saying, ‘Go, dwell among the sons of the nations that serve idols.’

וּכְעַן לָא יִתְשַׁד דָמִי לְאַרְעָא מִן קֳדָם מֵימְרָא דַייָ אֲרֵי נְפַק מַלְכָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִיבְעֵי יַת חֲלָשׁ חַד כְּמָא דְמִתְרַדֵיף קוֹרָאָה בְּטוּרַיָא:


And now, let not my blood be shed on the earth, away from before the speech of the LORD, for the king of Israel has come out to seek one weak person, as a partridge is hunted in the mountains.”

Then Saul said, “I am guilty. Return, my son David, for I will no more do you harm, because my soul was precious in your sight this day. Behold, I have been foolish and behaved exceedingly obtusely.”

And David answered and said, “Behold the spear of the king. Let one of the young men come over and fetch it.

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 27:1
