And David said in his heart, “Now, perhaps I shall be delivered one day into the hand of Saul. There is nothing better for me than that I should indeed escape to the land of the Philistines. Then Saul will rest from seeking me any longer within the borders of the land of Israel and I shall escape from his hand.”

So David arose and went over, he and the six hundred men who were with him, to Achish the son of Maoch, king of Gath.

And David dwelt with Achish at Gath, he and his men; each man with the men of his house, David with his two wives, Ahinoam who was from Jezreel, and Abigail, Nabal’s widow, who was from Carmel.

And when it was told Saul that David had fled to Gath, he sought him no more.

And the number of the days that David dwelt in the cities of the Philistines was a year and four months.

Now David and his men went up, and marched out against the Geshurites, the Gizrites, and the Amalekites, for these were the inhabitants of the land, which was from of old the entrance of the Heger unto the land of Egypt.

And David used to strike the inhabitants of the land and he left neither man nor woman alive, but captured the sheep, the oxen, the asses, the camels, and the clothes, and came back to Achish.

When Achish asked, “Where have you marched today?” David would say, “Against the south of Judah,” or, “Against the south of the Jerahmeelite,” or, “Against the south of the Shalmaite.”

And David left neither man nor woman alive to bring them to Gath, thinking, “Lest they should tell about us, and say, ‘So David has done.’” Such was his custom all the days he dwelt in the cities of the Philistines.

וְהֵימִין אָכִישׁ בְּדָוִד לְמֵימָר אִתְגְרָאָה אִתְגָרֵי בְּעַמֵיהּ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהֵי לִי עֲבַד פְּלַח לַעֲלָם:


And Achish trusted David, thinking, “He has utterly provoked his own people, Israel. Now he shall be a servant, subjected to me forever.”



## Chapter 28

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:1)

In those days the Philistines gathered their forces for an army, to wage war against Israel. And Achish said to David, “Understand well that you are to go with me in the army, you and your men.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:2)

And David said to Achish, “Thus you shall know what your servant can do.” And Achish said to David, “Thus I will make you guardian of my head all the days.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 28:3

Then Saul said to his servants, “Seek out for me a woman who knows how to conjure up the spirits of the dead that I may go to her and inquire of her.” And his servants said to him, “Behold, there is a woman at Endor who knows how to conjure up the spirits of the dead.”

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:3)


וַאֲמַרוּ רַבְרְבֵי פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי מָה יְהוּדָאֵי הָאִלֵין וַאֲמַר אָכִישׁ לְרַבְרְבֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי הֲלָא דֵין דָוִד עַבְדָא דְשָׁאוּל מַלְכָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דַהֲוָה עִמִי דְנַן יוֹמִין אוֹ דְנַן שְׁנִין וְלָא אַשְׁכָּחִית בֵּיהּ מִדַעַם בִּישׁ מִיוֹמָא דְאִתְפְרַשׁ לְוָתִי עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין:


the commanders of the Philistines said, “Why are these Judaeans here?” And Achish said to the commanders of the Philistines, “Is not this David, the servant of Saul, king of Israel, who has been with me now for days, or rather for years? I have found nothing wrong in him since the day he departed to me to this day.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:4)


וּרְגִיזוּ עֲלוֹהִי רַבְרְבֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וַאֲמַרוּ לֵיהּ רַבְרְבֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי אָתֵיב יַת גַבְרָא וִיתוּב לְאַתְרֵיהּ דְמַנִיתֵיהּ תַּמָן וְלָא יֵחוֹת עִמָנָא בִּקְרָבָא וְלָא יְהֵי לְשָׂטָן בִּקְרָבָא וּבַמֶה יִתְרְעֵי דֵין לְוַת רִבּוֹנֵיהּ הֲלֹא בְּרֵישֵׁי גַבְרַיָא הָאִינוּן:


But the commanders of the Philistines were angry with him. And the commanders of the Philistines said to him, “Let the man return, that he may return to the place which you have appointed to him. He shall not go down with us to battle, lest in the battle he become an adversary to us. For how could this one please his lord? Would it not be with the heads of these men?


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:5)


הֲלָא דֵין דָוִד דְמַשְׁמְעִין לֵיהּ בְּחִנְגַיָא לְמֵימָר קְטַל שָׁאוּל בְּאַלְפִין וְדָוִד בְּרִבְוָן:


Is not this David, of whom they proclaimed in dances, ‘Saul has killed by the thousands and David by the ten thousands.’”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:6)


וּקְרָא אָכִישׁ לְדָוִד וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ קַיֵם הוּא יְיָ אֲרֵי כָשַׁר אַתְּ וְטַב בְּעֵינַי מַפְקָךְ וּמַעֲלָךְ עִמִי בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא אֲרֵי לָא אַשְׁכָּחִית בָּךְ מִדַעַם בִּישׁ מְיוֹמָא דַאֲתֵיתָא לְוָתִי עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין וּבְעֵינֵי טוּרְנַיָא לָא תַקֵין אָתְּ:


Then Achish called David and said to him, “As the LORD is the existing One, you have been capable, and it seemed right in my sight that you should go out and come in with me in the army, for I have found nothing wrong in you from the day of your coming to me to this day. But you seem not right in the sight of the chiefs.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:7)


וּכְעַן תּוּב וֶאֱזֵיל בִּשְׁלָם וְלָא תַעְבֵיד בִּישׁ בְעֵינֵי טוּרְנֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי:


So return and go in peace, that you may not do anything wrong in the sight of the chiefs of the Philistines.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:8)


וַאֲמַר דָוִד לְאָכִישׁ אֲרֵי מָה עֲבָדִית וּמַה אַשְׁכַּחְתָּא בְעַבְדָךְ מִיוֹמָא דַהֲוֵיתִי מְשַׁמֵשׁ קֳדָמָךְ עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין אֲרֵי לָא אֱהָךְ וְאַגִיח קְרָבָא בְּשַׂנְאֵי רִבּוֹנִי מַלְכָּא:


And David said to Achish, “But what have I done? What have you found in your servant from the day I commenced serving before you to this day, that I may not go and wage war against the haters of my lord the king?”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:9)


וַאֲתֵיב אָכִישׁ וַאֲמַר לְדָוִד יְדַעְנָא אֲרֵי תַקִין אַתְּ בְּעֵינַי כְּמַלְאָכָא דַייָ בְּרַם רַבְרְבֵי פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי אֲמַרוּ לָא יִסַק עִמָנָא בִּקְרָבָא:


And Achish answered and said to David, “I know that you are as right in my sight as an angel of the LORD. Nevertheless the commanders of the Philistines have said, ‘He shall not go up with us to the battle.’


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:10)


וּכְעַן אַקְדֵים בְּצַפְרָא וְעַבְדֵי רִבּוֹנָךְ דַאֲתוֹ עִמָךְ וּתְקַדְמוּן בְּצַפְרָא וְיִנְהַר לְכוֹן וְתֵיזְלוּן:


Now then rise early in the morning with the servants of your lord who came with you. And you shall rise early in the morning and go as soon as you have light.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:11)


וְאַקְדֵים דָוִד הוּא וְגַבְרוֹהִי לְמֵיזַל בְּצַפְרָא לְמֵתַב לַאֲרַע פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי סְלִיקוּ לְיִזְרְעֶאל:


So David rose early, he and his men, to go in the morning, to return to the land of the Philistines. But the Philistines went up to Jezreel.



## Chapter 30

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 30:1

Now when David and his men came to Ziklag on the third day – the Amalekites had marched out against the South and against Ziklag; they had struck Ziklag and burnt it with fire,

וּשְׁבוֹ יָת נְשַׁיָא דִי בָהּ מִזְעֵירָא וְעַד רַבָּא וְלָא קָטְלוּ אֱנַשׁ וּדְבָרוּ וַאֲזַלוּ לְאוֹרְחֵיהוֹן:


and they had taken captive the women who were in it, both small and great; without killing anyone, they led them off and went their way –


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:3)


וַאֲתָא דָוִד וְגַבְרוֹהִי לְקַרְתָּא וְהָא קַלְיָא בְּנוּרָא וּנְשֵׁיהוֹן וּבְנֵיהוֹן וּבְנָתֵיהוֹן אִשְׁתָּבִיאוּ:


when David and his men came to the city, behold, it was burnt with fire and their wives and sons and daughters were taken captive.

וַאֲרֵים דָוִד וְעַמָא דִי עִמֵיהּ יַת קַלְהוֹן וּבְכוֹ עַד דְלָא הֲוָה בְהוֹן חֵילָא לְמִבְכֵּי:


Then David and the people who were with him raised their voices and wept, until they had no more strength to weep.

וְתַרְתֵּין נְשֵׁי דָוִד אִשְׁתְּבִיאָה אֲחִינוֹעַם דְמִיזְרְעֶאל וַאֲבִיגַיִל אִתַּת נָבָל דְמִכַּרְמְלָא:


David’s two wives also had been taken captive, Ahinoam who was from Jezreel and Abigail the widow of Nabal who was from Carmel.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:6)


וְעָקַת לְדָוִד לַחֲדָא אֲרֵי אֲמַרוּ עַמָא לְמִרְגְמֵיהּ אֲרֵי מָרַת נַפְשָׁא דְכָל עַמָא גְבַר עַל בְּנוֹהִי וְעַל בְּנָתֵיהּ וְאַתְקֵיף דָוִד בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ אֱלָהֵיהּ:


And David was greatly distressed, for the people spoke of stoning him, because all the people were bitter of soul each for his sons and daughters. But David strengthened himself in the speech of the LORD his God.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:7)


וַאֲמַר דָוִד לְאֶבְיָתָר כַּהֲנָא בַּר אֲחִימֶלֶךְ קָרִיב כְּעַן לִי אֵפוֹדָא וְקָרֵיב אֶבְיָתָר יַת אֵפוֹדָא לְדָוִד:


And David said to Abiathar the priest, the son of Ahimelech, “Bring me the ephod.” So Abiathar brought the ephod to David.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:8)


וּשְׁאֵיל דָוִד בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ לְמֵימָר אֶרְדוֹף בָּתַר מַשְׁרִיתָא הָדָא הֲאַדְבְּקִינֵיהּ וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ רְדוֹף אֲרֵי אַדְבֵּיק תַּדְבֵיק וְשֵׁיזָבָא תְּשֵׁיזֵיב:


And David inquired of the speech of the LORD, “I willpursue after this army. Shall I overtake them?” And he said to him, “Pursue, for you shall surely overtake and shall surely rescue.”

So David went, he and the six hundred men who were with him, and they came to the brook Besor. And part of them were left behind.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:10)

But David went on with the pursuit, he and four hundred men. Two hundred men, who refrained from crossing the brook Besor, stayed behind.

And they found an Egyptian man in the field and brought him to David. And they gave him bread and he ate, they gave him water to drink,

וִיהָבוּ לֵיהּ פִּילַס דְבֵלְתָּא וּתְרֵין אִיתְכְּלִין דְעִינְבִין יַבִּישִׁין וְאָכַל וְתָבַת רוּחֵיהּ לֵיהּ אֲרֵי לָא אֲכַל לַחְמָא וְלָא שָׁתָא מַיָא תְּלָתָא יְמָמִין וּתְלָתָא לֵילַוָן:


and they gave him a pouch of dried figs and two clusters of dried grapes. And when he had eaten, his spirit revived, for he had not eaten bread or drunk water for three days and three nights.

And David said to him, “To whom do you belong? And where are you from?” He said, “I am an Egyptian young man, servant to an Amalekite man. And my lord left me behind, because I fell sick – today three days ago.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:14)


אֲנַחְנָא אִתְנְגִידְנָא עַל דָרוֹמָא כְּרֵית וְעַל דִי לִיהוּדָה וְעַל דָרוֹם כָּלֵב וְיַת צִקְלַג אוֹקִידְנָא בְּנוּרָא:


We had marched out against the South of Chereth and against those of Judah and against the South of Caleb. And we burnt Ziklag with fire.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:15)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ דָוִד הֲתַחְתִּינַנִי לְמַשְׁרִיתָא הָדָא וַאֲמַר קַיָם לִי בְּמֵימְרָא דַייָ דְלָא תִקְטְלִינַנִי וְלָא תִמְסְרִינַנִי בִּידָא דְרִבּוֹנִי וְאַחְתִינָךְ לְמַשְׁרִיתָא הָדָא:


And when David said to him, “Will you take me down to this army?” he said, “Swear to me by the speech of the LORD that you will not kill me or surrender me into the hand of my lord. Then I will take you down to this army.”


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:16)


וְאַחְתֵיהּ וְהָא רְטִישִׁין עַל אַפֵּי כָל אַרְעָא אָכְלִין וְשָׁתָן וְחָגְגִין בְּכָל עֲדָאָה סַגִיאָה דִשְׁבוֹ מֵאַרְעָא פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וּמֵאַרְעָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה:


And when he had taken him down, behold, they were spread about over the surface of all the land, eating and drinking and feasting with the great booty they had taken from the land of the Philistines and from the land of the House of Judah.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:17)


וּמְחָנוּן דָוִד מִקְבָּלָא וְעַד עִדַן רַמְשָׁא בְּיוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹהִי וְלָא אִשְׁתֵּיזֵיב מִנְהוֹן אֱנַשׁ אֱלָהֵין אַרְבַּע מְאָה גְבַר עוּלֵים דִרְכִיבוּ עַל גַמְלַיָא וְאַפִּיכוּ:


And David struck them from the dark until the time of the evening of the day after. And not a man of them escaped, except four hundred young men, who mounted cow camels and retreated.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 30:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:18)


וְשֵׁיזִיב דָוִד יַת כָּל דִשְׁבוֹ עֲמַלְקָאֵי וְיַת תַּרְתֵּין נְשׁוֹהִי שֵׁיזִיב דָוִד:


David rescued all whom the Amalekites had taken captive and David rescued his two wives.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 30:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:19)


וְלָא שְׁגָא לְהוֹן מִן זְעִירָא וְעַד רַבָּא וְעַד בְּנִין וּבְנָן וּמִבִּיזְתָא וְעַד כָּל דִי נְסִיבוּ לְהוֹן כּוּלָא אָתִיב דָוִד:


Nothing was missing, whether small or great, sons or daughters or plunder; really anything that they had taken for themselves. David brought back all.

David also took captive all the sheep and cows. And they drove before those herds and said, “This is David’s booty!”

Then David came to the two hundred men who refrained from going after David and whom he had posted at the brook Besor. And they came out to meet David and to meet the people who were with him. And when David met the people, he asked them for their welfare.

Then all the bad and wicked among the men who had gone with David reacted and said, “Because they did not go with me, we will not give them any of the booty which we have rescued; except that every man may lead away his wife and sons, and depart.”

But David said, “You shall not do so, my brothers, [after] what the LORD has given us. He has preserved us and surrendered into our hand the army that came against us.

Who would listen to this proposal of yours? For as his share is who goes down into the battle, so shall his share be who stays by the baggage. They shall share alike.”

## Chapter 31

###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:1)


וּפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי מַגִיחִין קְרָבָא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל וְאַפִּיכוּ אֱנָשֵׁי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִן קֳדָם פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וּנְפַלוּ קְטִילִין בְּטוּרָא דְגִלְבּוֹעַ:


Now the Philistines were waging war in Israel and the men of Israel retreated from before the Philistines and fell killed on Mount Gilboa.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:2)


וְאַדְבִּיקוּ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי יַת שָׁאוּל וְיַת בְּנוֹהִי וּקְטָלוּ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי יַת יְהוֹנָתָן וְיַת אֲבִינָדָב וְיַת מַלְכִּי שׁוּע בְּנֵי שָׁאוּל:


And the Philistines overtook Saul and his sons. And the Philistines killed Jonathan and Abinadab and Malchishua, the sons of Saul.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:3)


וְתַקִיפוּ עָבְדֵי קְרָבָא עַל שָׁאוּל וְאַשְׁכְּחוֹהִי קַשְׁתַּיָא גַבְרִין דְאוּמָנִין לְמֵיגַד בְּקַשְׁתָּא וּדְחִיל לַחֲדָא מִקַשְׁתַּיָא:


The wagers of war pressed hard upon Saul and the archers found him, men who were skilled at bending the bow. And he feared greatly from the archers.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:4)


וַאֲמַר שָׁאוּל לְנָטֵל זְיָנֵיהּ שְׁלוֹף חַרְבָּךְ וְקַטְלַנִי בָהּ דִלְמָא יֵיתוּן עֲרֵלַיָא הָאִלֵין וְיִקְטְלוּנַנִי וְיִתְלַעֲבוּן בִּי וְלָא אָבָא נָטֵל זְיָנֵיהּ אֲרֵי דָחִיל לַחֲדָא וּנְסִיב שָׁאוּל יַת חַרְבָּא וּנְפַל עֲלָהּ:


Then Saul said to his armour bearer, “Draw your sword and kill me with it, lest these uncircumcised come and kill me and mock me.” But his armour bearer would not, for he feared greatly. So Saul took his own sword and fell upon it.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:5)


וַחֲזָא נָטֵל זְיָנֵיהּ אֲרֵי מִית שָׁאוּל וּנְפַל אַף הוּא עַל חַרְבֵּיהּ וּמִית עִמֵיהּ:


And when his armour bearer saw that Saul was dead, he also fell upon his sword and died with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:6)


וּמִית שָׁאוּל וּתְלָתָא בְּנוֹהִי וְנָטֵל זְיָנֵיהּ אַף כָּל גַבְרוֹהִי בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא כַּחֲדָא:


Thus Saul died, and his three sons, and his armour bearer, all his men on that very day together.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:7)


וַחֲזוֹ אֱנָשֵׁי יִשְׂרָאֵל דִבְּעִבְרָא דְמֵישְׁרָא וְדִבְּעִבְרָא דְיַרְדְנָא אֲרֵי אַפִיכוּ אֱנָשֵׁי יִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲרֵי מִיתוּ שָׁאוּל וּבְנוֹהִי וּשְׁבָקוּ יַת קִרְוַיָא וְאַפִּיכוּ וַאֲתוֹ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וִיתִיבוּ בְהוֹן:


And when the men of Israel, those on the other side of the valley and those on the other side of the Jordan, saw that the men of Israel had retreated and that Saul and his sons were dead, they left the cities and retreated. And the Philistines came and dwelt in them.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:8)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹהִי וַאֲתוֹ פְלִשְׁתָּאֵי לְחַלָצָא קְטִילַיָא וְאַשְׁכָּחוּ יַת שָׁאוּל וְיַת תְּלָתָא בְּנוֹהִי קַטִילִין רְמָן בְּטוּרָא דְגִלְבּוֹעַ:


On the day after, when the Philistines came to strip the killed ones, they found Saul and his three sons killed, lying on Mount Gilboa.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:9)


וּפְסַקוּ יַת רֵישֵׁיהּ וְחַלִיצוּ זְיָנֵיהּ וּשְׁלָחוּ בְּאַרְעָא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי סְחוֹר סְחוֹר לְבַסָרָא בֵּית טַעֲוַתְהוֹן וְיַת עַמָא:


And they cut off his head and stripped off his armour. And they sent [messengers] throughout the land of the Philistines to carry the good news into the house of their idols and to the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:10)


וְשַׁוִיאוּ יַת זְיָנֵיהּ בֵּית עַשְׁתְּרָתָא וְיַת גוּפֵיהּ צְלָבוּ בְּשׁוּרָא דְבֵית שָׁן:


They put his armour in the house of Ashtaroth and they hung his body on the wall of Beth shan.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:11)


וּשְׁמָעוּ עֲלוֹהִי יַתְבֵי יָבֵשׁ גִלְעָד יַת דַעֲבַדוּ פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי לְשָׁאוּל:


But when the inhabitants of Jabesh gilead heard about it – what the Philistines had done to Saul –


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:12)


וְקָמוּ כָּל גְבַר גִבַּר וַאֲזַלוּ כָּל לֵילְיָא וּנְסִיבוּ יַת גוּפָא דְשָׁאוּל וְיַת גוּפֵי בְּנוֹהִי מִשׁוּרָא דְבֵית שָׁן וַאֲתוֹ לְיָבֵשׁ וּקְלוֹ עֲלֵיהוֹן כְּמָא דְקָלַן עַל מַלְכַיָא תַּמָן:


all the heroic men arose and went all night. They took the body of Saul and the bodies of his sons from the wall of Beth shan. And when they came to Jabesh, they kindled for them a fire, as they used to kindle for the kings there.


###### Targum Jonathan on I Samuel 31:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:13)


וּנְסִיבוּ יַת גַרְמֵיהוֹן וּקְבָרוּ תְּחוֹת אֲשֵׁלָא בְּיָבֵשׁ וְצָמוּ שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין:


And they took their bones and buried them under the tamarisk tree in Jabesh, and fasted seven days.
